Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) probe the birth and evolution of black hole accretion flows and jets on human timescales. Radio emission traces shocks and outflows from thermal TDEs and powerful relativistic jets in the rare jetted class. SKA Mid, phased for VLBI and used together with global networks, will deliver milliarcsecond imaging, tens of microarcsecond astrometry, and microJy sensitivity, enabling: (i) proper motion measurements that discriminate off axis relativistic jets from subrelativistic winds; (ii) resolved morphologies and magnetic field diagnostics via polarimetry; and (iii) precise nuclear localization to distinguish SMBH vs. IMBH and to reveal recoiling or binary systems. SKA's wide frequency coverage (0.35 to 15.4 GHz) and 1h continuum sensitivities of 3 to 10 microJy per beam, together with multibeam tiedarray VLBI and a transient buffer for rapid triggers, are transformational. LSST, Einstein Probe, and SVOM will increase TDE alerts to hundreds per year, and late time radio flares appear common, ensuring rich SKA VLBI samples. We provide observing strategies, detection forecasts, and predictions, e.g., about 5 proper motion detections of jetted (or off axis) TDEs per year and routine core shift constraints at the microarcsecond level. This program will establish TDEs as laboratories for exploring jet launching, particle acceleration (including neutrinos), black hole accretion history and demographics, and properties of circumnuclear medium.

### [100] [Gamma-ray Bursts and Kilonovae from Gravitational Wave Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27037)
*Alberto Colombo,Marcello Giroletti,Susanna D. Vergani,Lauren Rhodes*

TL;DR: 本文概述了SKAO在引力波多信使天文学时代，特别是对GW电磁对应体（如千新星、GRB余辉）的射电观测能力和科学目标，包括对O5及后续运行周期的支持、GRB喷流结构研究以及独立于GW探测的GRB种群研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA进入新的观测周期（2028年O5）以及下一代探测器（爱因斯坦望远镜、宇宙探险家、LISA）即将到来，联合GW-电磁观测的需求日益增长。射电观测在GW170817中已被证明对约束抛射物性质至关重要，但现有射电望远镜的能力仍有局限，需要更强大的设施来系统性地搜寻和追踪GW对应体。

Method: 论文通过综述当前GW-电磁多信使观测格局，分析SKAO（特别是其初始配置AA*）在灵敏度、视场和长期监测能力方面的优势，阐述其在GW对应体射电搜寻、GRB余辉跟踪、喷流结构约束以及独立GW探测的GRB种群统计研究中的应用方法。

Result: SKAO将提供O5及后续周期所需的灵敏度和视场，实现GW对应体的长期射电监测；能够精确表征系统演化、详细探测GRB喷流结构、可能探测到速度降至非相对论性的偏离轴线喷流；独立于GW探测，还能开展长GRB（大质量星坍缩）和短GRB（中子星并合）的种群性质、喷流及环境研究。

Conclusion: SKAO将在多信使天文学时代发挥关键作用，其射电观测能力是识别和跟踪GW并合对应体最有效的方法，同时为GRB喷流结构和种群研究提供前所未有的机遇。

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves (GWs) from binary black holes in 2015 and the joint GW-electromagnetic (EM) observation of the binary neutron star merger GW170817 set a milestone in the multimessenger era in astrophysics. After four observing runs by the LIGO, Virgo, and KAGRA interferometers, a new cycle is planned for 2028, paving the way for next-generation detectors in the 2030s -- such as the Einstein Telescope, Cosmic Explorer, and LISA. The prospects for joint GW-EM studies, including kilonova searches in wide optical surveys, are vast but demanding. In the radio domain, connected interferometers and VLBI arrays have already proven essential in constraining the ejecta properties of GW170817. Radio emission from gamma-ray burst (GRB) afterglows, whether on- or off-axis, remains detectable for very long time, making radio observations the most effective method for identifying and tracking GW merger counterparts. These observations enable precise characterization of system evolution, detailed probing of GRB jet structures, and possible detection of misaligned jets once their velocity becomes non-relativistic. Even in its initial configuration (AA*), the SKAO will provide the sensitivity and field of view needed to complement GW counterpart searches during O5 and beyond, offering unmatched capabilities for long-term monitoring. Furthermore, independent of the GW detections, SKAO will enable population studies of the properties of both long (produced by the collapse of massive stars) and short (produced by the merger of neutron stars) GRBs, of their jets and of their environment. We present an overview of this evolving observational landscape and of the key scientific questions SKAO will address.

</details>

Torres,Javi Sánchez-Sierras,Antonio Martin-Carrillo,Thomas Wevers,Marco Berton,Minghao Yue,Vik S. Dhillon,Franz E. Bauer,Stuart P. Littlefair*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对8个快速X射线暂现源（FXT）进行深度光学观测，识别候选宿主星系并利用其性质（红移、恒星质量、恒星形成率、金属丰度、年龄）区分可能的起源机制。结果表明这些FXT可能来自多种起源，包括白矮星-中等质量黑洞潮汐瓦解事件、双中子星并合、超新星激波爆发等，并指出部分FXT与爱因斯坦探针探测到的塌缩星事件有所不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解密快速X射线暂现源（特别是缺乏多波段对应体的FXTs）的起源，理解它们的能量机制和环境。

Method: 对8个FXTs的位置进行深度光学地基观测，识别候选宿主星系；利用BAGPIPES能谱分布拟合代码推断每个候选宿主星系的光谱或测光红移、恒星质量、恒星形成率、金属丰度和恒星族年龄；通过宿主星系性质区分可能的 progenitors 场景。

Result: 识别出6个FXTs的候选宿主星系；确认XRT191223为银河系恒星耀发；多数FXTs的候选宿主星系性质与WD-IMBH TDE和BNS并合一致；两个与超新星激波爆发一致；EP240708a与长伽马暴的茧辐射一致；部分FXT的宿主星系可能未被探测到。

Conclusion: Chandra和XMM-Newton探测到的FXTs可能来自多种起源，且这部分群体与爱因斯坦探针探测到的多数符合塌缩星场景的FXTs有所不同。

Abstract: Fast X-ray transients (FXTs) are extragalactic flashes of X-rays with a typical duration of minutes to hours for which a variety of origins has been proposed and observed. To decipher the origin of FXTs, particularly those lacking multi-wavelength counterparts, we aim to understand their energetics and environments. We present deep optical ground-based observations of the positions of eight FXTs in order to try and identify and characterize candidate host galaxies. We use their properties to discriminate between possible progenitor scenarios. We identify candidate host galaxies for Swift~J050400.2+673405, XRT140507, XRT040610, XRT151121, XRT191127 and EP240708a. For each candidate, we infer the spectroscopic or photometric redshift, stellar mass, star formation rate, metallicity and stellar population age by fitting our data with the spectral energy distribution fitting code BAGPIPES. We re-identify XRT191223 as a Galactic stellar flare. For several FXTs, there are multiple candidate host galaxies, which complicates deriving constraints on the origin of the FXT. Assuming association with (one of) those candidates, all are consistent with a (non-)relativistic white dwarf - intermediate mass black hole tidal disruption event (WD-IMBH TDE) and a binary neutron star (BNS) merger. Two are consistent with a supernova shock breakout and only EP240708a with cocoon emission from a long gamma-ray burst. We also discuss the possibility that the host galaxies remain undetected in our observations. We conclude that FXTs detected by Chandra and XMM-Newton are likely to arise from a variety of origins, and we discuss that part of this population differs from FXTs detected by Einstein Probe many of which appear consistent with a collapsar scenario.

</details>


### [102] [Stellar black hole binaries from two common envelope evolution phases in triple stellar systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27193)
*Lotem Unger,Noam Soker*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三星演化通道，通过两次公共包层演化阶段形成双黑洞系统，其有效自旋参数χeff平均为正且存在负值尾部，与引力波观测一致。该通道可显著贡献观测到的并合双黑洞种群，并解释其自旋分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释引力波探测器观测到的并合双黑洞系统有效自旋χeff平均为正、存在负值尾部这一分布特征的形成机制。

Method: 提出三星演化通道：首次公共包层演化中第三星旋入并加速第一颗黑洞前身星的核自旋；第二次公共包层演化中第二颗星包裹黑洞并超新星爆发形成第二颗黑洞。采用经验标定的三星系统初始分布并施加层级稳定性判据。

Result: 预测的并合双黑洞与超新星爆发比率比观测值高出最多2倍；该通道能显著贡献观测到的并合双黑洞种群并解释其定性自旋分布。

Conclusion: 三星演化通道是形成观测到的并合双黑洞系统的重要途径，能够自然解释其平均正自旋与负自旋尾部的分布特征。

Abstract: We propose a triple-star evolutionary channel involving two common envelope evolution (CEE) phases to form close binary black hole (BBH) systems with an average positive effective inspiral spin $χ_{\rm eff}$ and a tail of systems having $χ_{\rm eff}<0$, as observed by gravitational wave detectors. $χ_{\rm eff}$ is the mass-weighted spin of the two merging BHs, and a positive (negative) value is for an effective spin along (opposite) the orbital angular momentum. The first BH progenitor engulfs a low-mass star during the post-main-sequence evolution. The tertiary star spirals in and spins up the core, which forms the first BH at the first core-collapse supernova (CCSN) explosion. Its spin is along the orbital angular momentum of the inner binary, which can be highly inclined to the outer binary angular momentum. The secondary star later engulfs the BH in a second CEE phase and explodes as a CCSN to form the second BH with a spin that is more aligned with the orbital angular momentum of the two BHs. We use empirically calibrated initial distributions of triple-star systems consisting of two massive stars and impose a hierarchical stability criterion. We compare the predicted ratio of merging BBHs to CCSN explosion rates and find it is up to a factor of 2 larger than the observed rate. This channel can significantly contribute to the population of observed merging BBHs and can explain their qualitative spin distribution.

</details>


### [103] [A search for Fast Radio Bursts from globular clusters in M49 with FAST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27225)
*Simon C. -C. Ho,Chris Flynn,Matthew Bailes,Emma Carli,Lei Zhang,Manisha Caleb,Kenneth C. Freeman,Tetsuya Hashimoto,Tomotsugu Goto,James O. Chibueze*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了使用FAST望远镜对M49星系中约4230个球状星团进行9小时观测以搜寻快速射电暴（FRB）的结果，未检测到明确的FRB信号，并给出了流量密度灵敏度上限（约16.5 mJy）和FRB发生率上限（4.7 × 10^-4 FRB GC^-1 hr^-1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的起源仍不确定，磁星被认为是主要候选体。由于磁星主要通过年轻恒星群体的核心坍缩超新星形成，在古老恒星群体的球状星团中发现FRB 20200120E出乎意料。因此，扩大邻近星系FRB样本对于检验FRB在老年恒星环境中的形成通道至关重要。M49星系拥有约7000个球状星团，是开展球状星团FRB搜索的理想目标。

Method: 利用FAST 19波束接收器对M49进行了9小时SnapShotCal观测，覆盖约4230个球状星团（平均每个星团2.1小时），在0-5000 pc cm^-3色散量范围内进行了全面的单脉冲搜索。

Result: 未探测到确定的天体物理FRB。最显著触发事件的后处理信噪比为8.6 sigma，色散量412.2 pc cm^-3，但在考虑误报率后与热噪声统计一致。束平均峰值流量密度灵敏度约为16.5 mJy（对应1 ms爆发的流量密度限值约16.5 mJy ms），FRB发生率上限为4.7 × 10^-4 FRB GC^-1 hr^-1。

Conclusion: 该非探测结果仅限定了在该观测窗口内、高于该流量密度阈值的亮FRB事件。这一结果对理解FRB在老年恒星环境中的形成通道提供了观测约束。

Abstract: The origins of fast radio bursts (FRBs) remain uncertain, although magnetars are a leading progenitor candidate. Because magnetars are thought to form primarily through core-collapse supernovae in young stellar populations, the discovery of FRB 20200120E in a globular cluster (GC) in the nearby galaxy M81 was unexpected given the ancient stellar populations of GCs. Expanding the sample of FRBs localised to nearby galaxies is therefore essential for testing FRB formation channels in old stellar environments. M49 (NGC 4472) is a nearby (~17 Mpc), radio-quiet giant elliptical galaxy in the Virgo cluster hosting about 7000 GCs, making it an ideal target for GC FRB searches. We conducted a 9-hour SnapShotCal observation of M49 using the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical Telescope (FAST) 19-beam receiver, covering approximately 4230 GCs (2.1 hr per GC), and performed a comprehensive single-pulse search over a dispersion measure range of 0-5000 pc cm^-3. No unambiguous astrophysical FRBs were detected. The most significant trigger reached a post-processed signal-to-noise ratio of 8.6 sigma at a dispersion measure of 412.2 pc cm^-3, but is statistically consistent with thermal noise after accounting for the false-alarm rate. We derive a beam-averaged peak flux-density sensitivity of about 16.5 mJy (corresponding to a fluence limit of about 16.5 mJy ms for a 1 ms burst) and place an upper limit on the FRB occurrence rate of 4.7 x 10^-4 FRB GC^-1 hr^-1. Our non-detection constrains only bright bursts above this fluence threshold during the observing window.

</details>


### [104] [Origins of Cosmic Rays in the Galactic-extragalactic Transition Energy Range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27270)
*A. Corstanje,S. Saha,S. Bouma,J. Bray,S. Buitink,V. de Henau,E. Dickinson,B. Hare,A. Haungs,H. He,J. Hörandel,T. Huege,C. James,P. Laub,X. Li,H-J. Mathes,K. Mulrey,A. Nelles,F. Schlüter,O. Scholten,R. Spencer,C. Sterpka,K. Terveer,S. Thoudam,G. Trinh,P. Turekova,D. Veberic,K. Watanabe,C. Zhang,P. Zhang,Y. Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用SKA-Low射电望远镜结合粒子探测器阵列，测量银河系最高能量宇宙射线（10^16-10^18 eV）的质量成分，以解决高能宇宙射线起源和银河系-河外源过渡能量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能宇宙射线科学中的一个未解问题是：银河系源能加速的最高能量宇宙射线的起源，以及超过此能量后只有河外源能提供宇宙射线的过渡能量。测量质量成分对于将测量结果与源和传播模型进行比较至关重要。

Method: 基于LOFAR十多年的宇宙射线测量经验，提出利用SKA-Low射电望远镜，并增配小型粒子探测器阵列，通过射电探测技术测量10^16至10^18 eV能量范围内宇宙射线的质量成分。

Result: 研究表明SKA-Low结合粒子探测器阵列能够很好地推进该领域，实现对该能量范围内宇宙射线质量成分的测量。

Conclusion: SKA-Low结合粒子探测器阵列是测量银河系最高能量宇宙射线质量成分的有效工具，有助于揭示高能宇宙射线的起源和过渡能量问题。

Abstract: Cosmic rays arrive at Earth with energies ranging from $10^9$ to over $10^{20}$ eV. One of the open questions in high-energy cosmic ray science concerns the origin of the highest-energy cosmic rays that can be accelerated by Galactic sources, and the transition energy beyond which only extragalactic sources can provide. Measuring the mass composition gives essential information for comparing measurements to source and propagation models, both from the abundances at the source and from the maximum attainable energy which is proportional to the particle charge (and hence its mass). The highest-energy cosmic rays from the Galaxy are found in a range of $10^{16}$ to $10^{18}$ eV which is well suited for radio detection. Building on a decade of experience in measuring cosmic rays at LOFAR, we show that SKA-Low, augmented with an array of small particle detectors, is well suited to advance the field by measuring the mass composition of cosmic rays across this energy range.

</details>

### [105] [Optimal homological vanishing: cancellation of character sums and Patterson's conjecture over $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26440)
*Zhao Yu Ma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了函数域上算术和与辫向量空间同调群 $H_i(B_n, V^{\otimes n})$ 的关系，证明了依赖于有限 $n$ 的同调消没线，且其斜率随 $n$ 增大收敛到最优斜率。应用包括：推广了Patterson猜想（高阶Gauss和的偏倚上界）并猜想其在素幂阶时最优；以及证明在Galois $G$-扩张上，几乎所有特征和都呈现近平方根消没。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域上的许多算术和可以表示为 $H_i(B_n, V^{\otimes n})$ 的形式，而这些同调群的消没线能给出算术和的幂次消没（power-savings cancellation）。需要证明一个显式的消没线，并研究其斜率的最优性。

Method: 通过辫向量空间 $V$ 的同调性质，建立 $H_i(B_n, V^{\otimes n})$ 的消没线，并分析其斜率随 $n$ 增大的收敛行为。将理论应用于两类算术和：高阶Gauss和的偏倚上界（推广Patterson猜想）和Galois $G$-扩张上的特征和。

Result: 证明了依赖于有限 $n$ 的同调消没线，且其斜率收敛到最优斜率。对高阶Gauss和得到偏倚上界；对Galois扩张上的特征和证明了几乎处处近平方根消没。

Conclusion: 本文的理论框架统一了函数域上两类重要算术和的消没估计，所得消没线斜率渐进最优，并提出了关于素幂阶Gauss和偏倚的精确猜想。

Abstract: Many arithmetic sums over function fields can be expressed in terms of $H_i(B_n, V^{\otimes n})$ for some braided vector space $V$, and a vanishing line for these homology groups gives power-savings cancellation for the arithmetic sum. We prove an explicit vanishing line for $H_i(B_n,V^{\otimes n})$ depending only on the homology up to some finite $n$. Moreover, as the range of $n$ increases, the slope of the resulting vanishing line converges to the optimal slope. We also apply our methods to two different families of arithmetic sums. Firstly, we prove an upper bound for the bias of higher order Gauss sums over function fields, extending Patterson's conjecture beyond the cubic and quartic cases over number fields, and we conjecture this bound is sharp for orders that are prime powers. Secondly, we show that over Galois $G$-extensions, almost all character sums exhibit near square-root cancellation.

</details>


### [106] [Rank-Two Drinfeld Module over Elliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26851)
*Chuangqiang Hu,Ze-Dong Huang,Chang-An Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次显式构造了椭圆曲线坐标环上的秩二Drinfeld模，给出了其Anderson motive的生成元与二次τ-关系，并确定了模空间为超奇异曲线$Y^{q+1} = π(X)$内的开区域$Y \neq 0$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drinfeld模在Langlands纲领等领域至关重要，但其在非多项式坐标环上的显式构造长期悬而未决——除经典的$\mathbb{F}_q[t]$情形及Green-Papanikolas的秩一结果外，秩二情形此前完全无法处理。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在代数闭$\mathbf{A}$-域$L$上，基于秩一框架发展秩二符号规范化$\mathbf{A}$-Drinfeld模的显式理论；利用底层椭圆曲线的几何参数，推导Anderson motive的三个生成元及其封闭形式的二次τ-关系。

Result: 证明了Anderson motive $M_φ$由三个元素生成并满足单一二次τ-关系（闭形式）；模空间为超奇异曲线$Y^{q+1} = π(X)$中$Y \neq 0$的开区域，其中$π$为次数$2q+1$的显式多项式。

Conclusion: 成功解决了椭圆曲线坐标环上秩二Drinfeld模的显式构造问题，为超越多项式环情形的更高秩推广提供了系统性方法论。

Abstract: Drinfeld modules, introduced by D.~V.~Drinfeld in the 1970s, were originally developed as a function field analogue of elliptic curves and have since become a central tool in the Langlands program over function fields. The theory has been highly developed and shares deep connections with many areas, including algebraic geometry, number theory, and coding theory. Despite these advances, the explicit construction of Drinfeld modules over non-polynomial coordinate rings remains a largely open problem. Indeed, aside from the classical polynomial case $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$, explicit formulas for Drinfeld modules are known only in very limited situations. Let \(E\) be an elliptic curve over a finite field \(\mathbb{F}_q\) with a fixed rational point \(\infty\), and let \(\mathbf{A} = H^0(E\setminus\{\infty\}, \mathcal{O}_E)\) be its coordinate ring. Rank-one Drinfeld \( \mathbf{A} \)-modules were explicitly constructed by Green and Papanikolas, providing the first systematic example beyond the polynomial case. However, the rank-two case has remained completely inaccessible until now. This paper solves the rank-two case of this open problem in a fully explicit manner. Precisely, we develop an explicit theory for rank-two sign-normalized \(\mathbf{A}\)-Drinfeld modules over an algebraically closed \(\mathbf{A}\)-field \(L\), building upon the rank-one framework. We determine the structure of the associated Anderson motive \(M_φ\) and prove that it is generated by three elements subject to a single quadratic \(τ\)-relation, which we derive in closed form using the geometric parameters of the underlying elliptic curve.As a consequence, we find that the moduli space of sign-normalized rank-two Drinfeld \(\mathbf{A}\)-modules is an open domain $Y\neq 0 $ inside a supersingular curve \(Y^{q+1} = π(X) \), where \(π\) is an explicit polynomial of degree \(2q+1\).

</details>


### [107] [Computating decomposition groups and inertia groups using Newton polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26888)
*Kazuma Igarashi,Nozomu Suzuki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Kölle和Schmid的方法，在Montes和Nart引入的更弱假设（基于指数表述）下，展示了如何通过Newton多边形及相关数据确定分解群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人方法（Ore, Montes, Nart）利用Newton多边形计算素理想分解，Kölle和Schmid在Ore假设下能得到分解群。但更弱的Montes-Nart假设下尚无确定分解群的方法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文在Montes和Nart基于指数的假设框架下，扩展了Kölle和Schmid的方法，利用Newton多边形及关联数据来推导分解群的结构。

Result: 成功展示了在Montes-Nart的弱假设条件下，如何从Newton多边形和相关数据中确定分解群，将适用范围从Ore假设推广到更一般的场景。

Conclusion: 本文为在更宽松条件下利用Newton多边形确定分解群提供了完整方案，扩展了相关理论的应用范围。

Abstract: Newton polygons are powerful tools for computing the decomposition of prime ideals in extension rings. Methods based on Newton polygons have been developed through the work of Ore, Montes, and Nart. Kölle and Schmid obtained a method for determining decomposition groups from Newton polygons and associated data under assumptions introduced by Ore. In this paper, we extend their approach and show how to determine decomposition groups under the weaker assumptions introduced by Montes and Nart, formulated in terms of indices.

</details>


### [108] [Deterministic and Efficient Ideal Arithmetic via Two-Element Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26993)
*Qi Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个确定性的多项式时间算法，用于将数域中的理想表示为两个元素生成的形式，适用于范数与指数互素的情况，特别是单生成多项式情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数域中，许多场景需要将理想表示为环上两个元素生成的形式，但现有算法要么是随机化的，要么在密码学规模下不可行。

Method: 提出了一个确定性的多项式时间算法，核心技术是广义版本的Dedekind判别法，算法要求输入理想的范数与指数[O_K:Z[x]/f]互素。

Result: 该算法能够为范数与指数互素的理想找到双元素表示，特别适用于单生成多项式（包括格密码中广泛使用的分圆多项式）。

Conclusion: 提供了一个实用的、确定性的多项式时间算法来解决理想的二元素生成问题，弥补了现有随机化或不可行算法的不足。

Abstract: Given an ideal in a number field, it is desirable in many situations
  to find two elements that generate the ideal over the ring of the
  integers of the field. Existing algorithms are either randomized,
  or impractical at cryptographic sizes.
  In the paper, we present a deterministic
  polynomial time algorithm to find the two-element representation of
  an ideal. For a monic irreducible integral polynomial \( f(x) \),
  let \( K=\Q[x]/(f) \) be the number field, and
  \( O_K \) be the integral closure. Our algorithm works when
  the norm of the input ideal is co-prime to the index
  \( [O_K:\Z[x]/f] \). In particular, it
  handles all ideals for monogenic \( f(x) \),
  a class that includes the cyclotomic polynomials widely used in
  lattice based cryptography. A key technical ingredient in our
  result is a generalized version of Dedekind criterion.

</details>


### [109] [Averaged Fourier Estimates and Dyadic Approximation on the Cantor set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27034)
*Prasuna Bandi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Cantor集上关于2^n x的丢番图逼近的零一律问题，证明了当τ>2-γ时测度为0，当τ<(1-γ)/2时测度为1，推进了Velani猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决或推进Velani关于中间三分Cantor集中二进逼近的零一律猜想。

Method: 利用测度论与丢番图逼近方法，分析Cantor集上\|2^n x\|的无穷多次小值事件，通过阈值τ的临界值刻画零一律。

Result: 当τ>2-γ时，使得\|2^n x\|<n^{-τ}无穷多次成立的x的测度为0；当τ<(1-γ)/2时，测度为1。

Conclusion: 给出了Velani猜想的部分进展，确定了零一律的两个阈值边界。

Abstract: Let $C$ be the middle-third Cantor set and let $μ$ be the natural Cantor probability measure. Let \[ γ=\frac{\log2}{\log3}. \] The two main results of this paper are \[ μ\{x\in C:\|2^n x\|<n^{-τ}\text{ for infinitely many }n\}=0 \qquad \text{ for } τ>2-γ. \] and \[ μ\{x\in C:\|2^n x\|<n^{-τ}\text{ for infinitely many }n\}=1 \qquad \text{ for } τ<\frac{1-γ}{2}. \] These results give new progress toward Velani's conjecture on zero-one law for dyadic approximation in the middle-third Cantor set.

</details>


### [110] [Adelic Line Bundles, Arithmetic Positivity and Diophantine Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27116)
*Xinyi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了拟射影簇上的adelic线丛基础理论、算术正性概念及其在等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为拟射影簇上的adelic线丛提供系统性的基础阐述，并展示算术正性理论在等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想中的关键应用。

Method: 采用阐述性（expository）写作方式，系统整理adelic线丛的基础理论，包括定义、性质及其算术正性的刻画，并以此为基础推导等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想。

Result: 建立了拟射影簇上adelic线丛的完整理论框架，证明了算术正性与等分布定理、一致Bogomolov猜想之间的内在联系。

Conclusion: Adelic线丛的算术正性理论是连接算术几何与丢番图逼近的重要工具，在等分布和Bogomolov猜想等核心问题中具有深刻应用价值。

Abstract: This article is an expository account of the basics of adelic line bundles on quasi-projective varieties, arithmetic positivity of adelic line bundles, and applications of positivity to an equidistribution theorem and the uniform Bogomolov conjecture.

</details>


### [111] [Quantitativity in the Mordell Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27129)
*Xinyi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了Mordell猜想及其均匀性问题的最新进展，重点介绍了Yu-Yuan-Zhou最近工作所证明的定量版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mordell猜想断言：在数域上，亏格至少为2的光滑射影曲线上只有有限多个有理点。均匀Mordell问题进一步要求给出有理点数量的合适上界，该问题已通过Vojta、Dimitrov-Habegger-Gao和Kuhne的工作得到解决。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性文章，系统介绍了均匀Mordell问题的研究脉络，并重点阐述了Yu-Yuan-Zhou近期工作中证明的定量版本。

Result: 通过综述已有成果，展示了均匀Mordell问题从定性到定量的进展，特别是Yu-Yuan-Zhou工作给出了该问题的定量形式。

Conclusion: 这篇文章是对Mordell猜想均匀性问题最新进展的综述，重点介绍了Yu-Yuan-Zhou在定量版本上的贡献。

Abstract: The Mordell conjecture asserts that there are only finitely many rational points on a smooth projective curve of genus at least two over a number field. The uniform Mordell problem asks for suitable upper bounds on the number of rational points in the Mordell conjecture, and has been solved by combining works of Vojta, Dimitrov--Habegger--Gao and Kuhne. In this survey, we will introduce a quantitative version of the uniformity problem proved by the recent work of Yu--Yuan--Zhou.

</details>


### [112] [Determining Newforms via various relations among Fourier Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27190)
*Arvind Kumar,Moni Kumari,Prabhat Kumar Mishra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个新形式的傅里叶系数之间的算术关系如何用于确定其背后的模形式。对于扭转不等价的非CM新形式，获得了素数幂处归一化傅里叶系数的差、积和比值分布的定量结果。作为应用，推导了重数一定理的定量改进，并建立了Gafni--Thorner--Wong定理的密度一版本。进一步证明，对于扭转不等价的新形式，傅里叶系数的比值在实数轴上定量稠密，为通过系数比值分布确定新形式提供了新判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究如何利用两个新形式的傅里叶系数之间的算术关系来唯一确定其背后的模形式，特别是对扭转不等价的非CM新形式，建立定量的分布结果。

Method: 通过分析素数幂处归一化傅里叶系数的差、积和比值的分布，结合解析数论和模形式理论，建立定量稠密性和分布结果。

Result: 获得了扭转不等价非CM新形式傅里叶系数差值、乘积和比值的定量分布结果；推导了重数一定理的定量改进版本；建立了Gafni--Thorner--Wong定理的密度一版本；证明了系数比值在实数轴上的定量稠密性。

Conclusion: 傅里叶系数间的算术关系（特别是比值）可以定量地区分扭转不等价的新形式，提供了新的模形式确定性判据。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate how arithmetic relations among the Fourier coefficients of two newforms can be used to determine the underlying forms. For pairs of twist-inequivalent non-CM newforms, we obtain quantitative results on the distribution of differences, products, and ratios of normalized Fourier coefficients at prime powers. As applications, we derive quantitative refinements of multiplicity one and establish density one analogues of a theorem of Gafni--Thorner--Wong. We further show that, for twist-inequivalent newforms, the ratios of Fourier coefficients are quantitatively dense in the real line, which provides a new criterion for determining newforms through the distribution of their coefficient ratios.

</details>


### [113] [The Bogomolov Property through Galois Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27203)
*Lea Terracini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用Galois表示研究代数扩张的Bogomolov性质（即单位根群外绝对对数Weil高的正下界一致性）的框架、技术及成果，特别关注局部p-adic信息（如Sen定理）在证明新判据及应用于具有大局部像的表示中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogomolov性质最初作为Northcott性质的弱化引入，并与Lehmer猜想密切相关。本文的动机在于通过Galois表示的视角重新诠释经典结果，并将其推广到其他几何及非几何起源的Galois表示上，从而建立更一般的判据。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法，系统梳理了利用Galois表示研究Bogomolov性质的框架，重点介绍了如何将Sen定理（关于完全分歧的p-adic Lie扩张）融入新判据的证明，并应用于模表示及具有大局部像的表示。

Result: 给出了基于Galois表示的Bogomolov性质新判据，这些判据成功应用于模表示等情形，尤其依赖于局部p-adic信息；同时展示了该方法如何统一并推广已有的经典结果（如abelian扩张和椭圆曲线扭点生成的域）。

Conclusion: 通过Galois表示的视角，Bogomolov性质的研究得以系统化，局部p-adic信息（特别是Sen定理）在其中起到关键作用；该框架为判断更广泛的代数扩张是否具有Bogomolov性质提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The Bogomolov property \B for an algebraic extension of \(\QQ\) asserts the existence of a uniform positive lower bound for the absolute logarithmic Weil height outside the group of roots of unity. Originally introduced as a weakening of Northcott's property and closely related to Lehmer's conjecture, it has been established for several natural classes of infinite extensions, including abelian extensions and fields generated by torsion points of elliptic curves defined over the rationals.
  Given a Galois representation of an absolute Galois group, one can associate with it the algebraic extension fixed by its kernel and ask whether this extension has property \B. This point of view allows one to reinterpret classical results, and to generalize them to other Galois representations, both of geometric and non-geometric origin.
  This expository paper gives a survey of the techniques and results in this framework. We present some results on modular representations, with a particular focus on the role of local \(p\)-adic information. We explain how Sen's theorem on totally ramified \(p\)-adic Lie extensions enters the proof of new criteria for the Bogomolov property, and how these criteria apply to representations with large local image.
  This contribution is based on joint work with Francesco Amoroso, Andrea Conti, and Pietro Piras.

</details>


### [114] [A direct proof of Mono-Rolen-Stumpenhusen and new constructions via the Maass raising operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27212)
*Kathrin Bringmann,Ben Kane*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用微分算子（Maass上升算子）给出了Mono、Rolen和Stumpenhusen主要结果的直接概念性证明，将函数ω_{k+1,D}实现为二次型Poincaré级数f_{k,D}的Maass上升算子像，自然地解释了其模性和Laplace特征值性质，并进一步研究了更一般的局部Maass形式在Maass升降算子下的像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Mono、Rolen和Stumpenhusen的主要结果提供一个更直接、更具概念性的证明，并揭示函数ω_{k+1,D}与二次型Poincaré级数f_{k,D}之间的内在联系。

Method: 使用微分算子（Maass上升算子）将函数ω_{k+1,D}实现为二次型Poincaré级数f_{k,D}的像，并进一步研究更一般的局部Maass形式在Maass升降算子下的性质。

Result: 成功给出了原结果的直接概念性证明，自然解释了ω_{k+1,D}的模性和Laplace特征值性质，并将结果推广到更一般的局部Maass形式。

Conclusion: Maass算子方法为理解这些特殊函数的模性质提供了统一且自然的框架，且该方法可推广至更广泛的Maass形式类。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a direct conceptual proof of the main result of Mono, Rolen, and Stumpenhusen, using differential operators. More precisely, we realize their functions $ω_{k+1,D}$ as images of the quadratic form Poincaré series $f_{k,D}$ under the Maass raising operator. This perspective gives a natural explanation for the modularity and Laplace eigenvalue prop erties of $ω_{k+1,D}$. We further extend these results by investigating the images of more general local Maass forms under the Maass raising and lowering operators.

</details>


### [115] [Weighted Fruit Diophantine Equations and Hyperelliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27322)
*Jewel Mahajan,Apeksha Sanghi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究加权水果丢番图方程 $ax^{d} - c\bigl(m^{2}y^{2}+n^{2}z^{2}\bigr) + xyz - b = 0$，推广了 Majumdar--Sury、Vaishya--Sharma 和 Prakash--Chakraborty 的工作。在特定参数假设下，主要结果表明对于任何素数 $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ 且 $b = a (2 c m n)^{d} - l\, c^{s}t^{2q}$，该方程除 $x$ 模 $4l$ 的某些剩余类外无整数解。当 $l$ 被其奇次幂替换时也有类似结果。作者证明了 $l=-1$ 时的不可解性，并将主要结果应用于小素数 $l \in \{3, 7, 11, 19\}$，显式确定了无解情况外的例外剩余类。对于 $l=3$，得到了完全不可解性。还考虑了方程的更一般变体并给出不可解性结果，建立了正解的上界，并通过关联超椭圆曲线族和 Grant 的 Nagell--Lutz 定理类比，将不可解性转化为有理扭转点的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广前人在加权丢番图方程上的工作，探索更一般形式的加权水果方程在特定参数条件下的可解性，并建立系统的不可解性理论。

Method: 通过模 $4l$ 的同余分析法、素数 $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ 的二次剩余性质、参数特殊构造（$b$ 的特定形式）、超椭圆曲线关联及 Grant 的 Nagell--Lutz 定理类比。

Result: 对 $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ 的素数和特定 $b$，方程除 $x$ 模 $4l$ 的某些剩余类外无整数解；$l=3$ 时完全不可解；小素数情形显式给出例外类；推广变体也有不可解性；正解有上界；不可解性转化为超椭圆曲线的有理扭转点信息。

Conclusion: 建立了加权水果丢番图方程在特定参数下的系统不可解性结果，并通过与超椭圆曲线扭点的联系开辟了新的研究视角。

Abstract: We study the weighted fruit Diophantine equation $ax^{d} - c\bigl(m^{2}y^{2}+n^{2}z^{2}\bigr) + xyz - b = 0$, generalising previous work by Majumdar--Sury, Vaishya--Sharma, and Prakash--Chakraborty. Subject to specific hypotheses on the parameters, our main result shows that for any prime $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ and $b = a (2 c m n)^{d} - l\, c^{s}t^{2q}$, the above equation has no integer solutions except for certain residue classes of $x$ modulo $4l$. An analogous result also holds when $l$ is replaced by an odd power of $l$ in the definition of $b$. We prove some insolvability results for $l=-1$. By applying the main result to the small values of $l$, such as $l \in \{3, 7, 11, 19\}$, we explicitly determine the exceptional residue classes outside of which the equation has no solutions. In particular, for $l = 3$, this yields complete insolvability, and weakening these hypotheses still yields non-existence results, though with specific coprimality restrictions on any possible solutions. We also consider a more general variant of the above Diophantine equation and provide some insolvability results. Subsequently, we establish bounds for the positive solutions of the aforementioned equation. Finally, by associating a family of hyperelliptic curves with the equation under consideration and applying Grant's analogue of the Nagell--Lutz theorem, we translate these insolvability results into results about their rational torsion points.

</details>


### [116] [Amplified moments of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27323)
*Benjamin Durkan,Timothy Page*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了黎曼ζ函数二片放大第二和第四矩的渐近公式，并由此推导出多个联合矩的无条件有效下界，特别得到了第六矩的下界 M₃(T) ≥ (34.1+o(1))c₃T(log T)⁹，改进了Soundararajan的结果，去除了对Lindelöf假设的依赖，并得到了与随机矩阵理论预测一致的整数联合矩有效下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立黎曼ζ函数矩的精确下界，验证Keating–Wei和Keating–Snaith关于ζ函数矩的猜想，并移除先前工作中对Lindelöf假设的依赖。

Method: 采用二片放大（two-piece amplification）技术，建立ζ函数第二矩和第四矩的渐近公式，并利用这些公式推导联合矩的下界。

Result: 获得了第六矩M₃(T)≥(34.1+o(1))c₃T(log T)⁹的无条件有效下界，改进了Soundararajan的界；得到了所有整数联合矩与随机矩阵理论预测一致的有效下界。

Conclusion: 所得到的下界与Keating–Wei和Keating–Snaith的猜想高度吻合，且不依赖任何未证明的假设，为黎曼ζ函数矩的精确阶提供了坚实的无条件证据。

Abstract: We establish asymptotic formulae for two-piece amplified second and fourth moments of the Riemann zeta function. As applications, we obtain unconditional effective lower bounds for several joint moments of zeta which are in strong agreement with the conjectures of Keating--Wei and Keating--Snaith. In particular, we prove an unconditional lower bound for the sixth moment of zeta $M_3(T)\geq(34.1+o(1))c_3T(\log T)^9$. We further improve some of the lower bounds obtained by Soundararajan, removing the assumption of the Lindelőf Hypothesis, and we obtain effective lower bounds for all joint integer moments of zeta consistent with the predictions of random matrix theory.

</details>

### [117] [The fate of odd-parity magnetism in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26222)
*Kasper Rettedal Eikeland,Sondre Duna Lundemo,Asle Sudbø*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在玻色化框架下研究p波磁体的准一维模型，揭示局域矩的奇宇称磁序与巡游电子填充之间的可公度条件，以及该条件如何赋予电子谱函数显著的p波特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索p波磁体（奇宇称磁体）在准一维体系中的低能物理行为，特别是局域磁矩序与巡游电子之间的相互作用如何影响电子性质。

Method: 采用玻色化方法处理由扩展Hubbard模型（巡游电子）与Kondo耦合（局域矩）构成的耦合系统；从局域矩的经典基态出发，得到Luttinger液体的描述，进而分析弱耦合相图。

Result: 自旋链展现出与联合时间反演-平移对称性一致的准长程序；该序建立了电子填充的可公度条件，在此条件下玻色场论中出现额外相互作用，赋予电子谱函数明显的p波特征；离开可公度填充时，这些相互作用变得无关，p波特征消失。

Conclusion: p波磁体的奇宇称磁序与电子填充之间存在关键的可公度性关系，这一关系决定了电子谱函数是否呈现p波特征，为理解p波磁体中电子行为的微观机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider a one-dimensional model for a $p$-wave magnet within the bosonization framework. The model consists of itinerant electrons described by an extended Hubbard model coupled to a chain of localized moments through the Kondo exchange. The classical ground state of the local-moment system captures the salient features of an odd-parity magnet. By bosonizing the coupled system, a description in terms of coupled Luttinger liquids follows, giving rise to a rich weak-coupling phase diagram. It is shown that the spin chain develops quasi long-range order consistent with the combined time-reversal and translation symmetry defining the $p$-wave magnet. We highlight the peculiar role played by this order in establishing a commensurability condition on the electronic filling under which additional interactions appear in the bosonic field theory. It is demonstrated that these interactions endow the electron spectral function with a pronounced $p$-wave character. Away from commensurate filling, these interactions are rendered irrelevant and the ensuing $p$-wave character is lost.

</details>


### [118] [Toric code made subsystem: a framework for topological subsystem codes using anticommuting quantum spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26226)
*Vaibhav Sharma,Sumiran Pujari*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反交换量子自旋液体的拓扑子系统码构建框架。该框架通过空间修改环面码，利用其广泛的反对易局部守恒算符导致的基态简并作为子系统自由度，构建了拓扑子系统码。在方格和kagome格子上给出了具体例子，分别需要权重4和权重3的局部校验算符测量。与其它子系统码不同，该码具有大量不被校验算符干扰的局域规范量子比特。该框架可推广到不同晶格或图几何，适用于多种量子硬件平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑子系统码种类有限，缺乏基于反交换量子自旋液体的构造方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用反交换量子自旋液体中广泛的基态简并和拓扑序，构建新型拓扑子系统码，为量子纠错提供更多选择。

Method: 以反交换量子自旋液体的规范模型为起点，该模型是环面码的空间修改版本，失去了稳定子码性质但具有广泛的反对易局部守恒算符。利用这些算符导致的基态简并作为子系统自由度，构建拓扑子系统码。在方格（权重4校验算符）和kagome晶格（权重3校验算符）上给出具体构造实例。

Result: 成功构建了基于反交换量子自旋液体的拓扑子系统码，在方格和kagome晶格上给出了具体实现。该码具有独特的性质：存在大量不受校验算符干扰的局域规范量子比特，且继承了量子自旋液体的多体拓扑序。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可推广的模板，用于在不同晶格或图几何上生成新型拓扑子系统码，适合在多种量子硬件平台上实现，扩展了拓扑子系统码的种类和应用范围。

Abstract: We introduce a framework of constructing topological subsystem codes based on the class of anticommuting quantum spin liquids described in [Phys. Rev. B 113, 064402 (2026)]. A canonical model from this class can be considered as a spatial modification of the toric code that voids its stabilizer code property. Rather, these models contain an extensive set of anticommuting local conserved operators that lead to an extensive ground state degeneracy. This degeneracy forms the basis of the subsystem degrees of freedom in the associated quantum error correcting code. The code inherits the many-body topological order of the quantum spin liquid, making it a topological subsystem code. We present two concrete and detailed examples for constructing these codes on a square lattice and a kagome lattice geometry, requiring weight-4 and weight-3 local check operator measurements respectively. In contrast to other subsystem codes, a unique property of these codes is the presence of an extensive number of local gauge qubits that are left undisturbed by the check operators apart from the logical qubits. Our construction provides a template for generating this new category of topological subsystem codes on different lattice or graph geometries, suitable for implementation on various quantum hardware platforms.

</details>


### [119] [Bobkingite, a new coupled sawtooth chain platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26243)
*P. Peter Stavropoulos,Aleksandar Razpopov,Harrison LaBollita,Michael R. Norman,Antia S. Botana,Roser Valentí*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究矿物bobkingite作为锯齿链（sawtooth chain）模型的潜在实现材料，通过第一性原理计算发现其具有准一维锯齿链结构，展现出丰富的简并态和潜在的经典自旋液体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找锯齿链自旋系统的真实材料实现，探索bobkingite中独特的磁交换耦合网络及低维磁性行为。

Method: 采用第一性原理（ab initio）方法估算磁交换耦合；使用自旋波理论分析量子涨落效应；通过经典模型研究简并态流形。

Result: Bobkingite中磁交换耦合形成准一维锯齿链结构，三维耦合被晶体几何强烈抑制；经典模型存在具有二维特征的大面积近简并态；量子涨落下这些简并态以伊辛自由度的形式保持；bobkingite的垂直链间耦合可忽略，即使在有序态中也保持一维简并性。

Conclusion: 热涨落可能稳定有限温经典自旋液体，降温时发生级联相变进入低维简并态，使bobkingite成为探索锯齿链物理的重要平台。

Abstract: We investigate the mineral bobkingite, \ce{Cu5(OH)8Cl2(H2O)2}, as a potential realization of the sawtooth chain. Using \textit{ab initio} methods, we estimate the magnetic exchange couplings and find that bobkingite hosts quasi-one-dimensional sawtooth chains, with residual three-dimensional interactions strongly suppressed by the crystal geometry. Examining the full exchange network, we find that the classical model exhibits an extensive manifold of nearly degenerate states with emergent two-dimensional character, which spin-wave theory shows to persist to leading order in quantum fluctuations as Ising degrees of freedom. Unlike other sawtooth candidates, bobkingite has negligible vertical interchain coupling, preserving a one-dimensional degeneracy even in the presence of ordering, suggesting that any long-range order is weak. Thermal fluctuations may thus stabilize a finite-temperature classical spin liquid regime, with a cascade of transitions upon cooling into successively lower-dimensional degenerate states, making bobkingite a compelling platform for exploring sawtooth chain physics.

</details>


### [120] [Odd-parity electronic order near the semiconductor limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26338)
*Jack Tregidga,Dibyata Rout,Johannes Hielscher,Josiah Turner,Stephen D. Wilson,John W. Harter*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在磷化物半导体家族LnCd₃P₃（Ln=La, Ce, Pr, Nd）中发现了稀薄载流子体系的自发奇宇称电子相变，该相变伴随体反演和旋转对称性破缺，仅出现在轻自掺杂空穴的化合物中，为稀载流子半导体中的相互作用驱动奇宇称破缺提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找稀薄载流子体系中强库仑相互作用的材料平台，探索超越常规费米液体范式的集体不稳定性（如超导、维格纳结晶、奇宇称电子序等），而晶体材料中这类稀薄、强相互作用的电子体系实验实现仍十分罕见。

Method: 利用光学二次谐波产生（SHG）探测对称性破缺，结合二次谐波显微镜观察畴结构，采用超快瞬态反射测量表征电子重构，并通过密度泛函理论（DFT）构建四带价态模型进行理论分析。

Result: 观察到自发体反演和旋转对称性破缺，出现面内极化轴；三种120°旋转相关的畴变体；相变伴随显著的电子重构；有序相仅出现在轻自掺杂空穴化合物中，绝缘性SmCd₃P₃中不出现；理论模型表明适度相互作用可稳定奇宇称电子序，该相包含自发费米面畸变和动量依赖的双层极化。

Conclusion: 建立了稀载流子半导体中相互作用驱动宇称破缺的途径，确定了蜂窝状双层体系作为奇宇称电子相的有前景平台。

Abstract: Identifying materials platforms in which dilute carriers experience strong Coulomb interactions is a central challenge in the search for interaction-driven quantum phases. In such systems, weak carrier screening can promote a variety of collective instabilities beyond the conventional Fermi liquid paradigm, including superconductivity, Wigner crystallization, and odd-parity electronic order. Experimental realizations of such dilute, strongly interacting electronic systems remain rare in crystalline materials. Here we report a spontaneous odd-parity phase transition in the phosphide semiconductor family $\textit{Ln}$Cd$_3$P$_3$ ($\textit{Ln}$ = La, Ce, Pr, Nd). Using optical second harmonic generation, we observe the onset of bulk inversion and rotational symmetry breaking accompanied by the emergence of an in-plane polar axis. Second harmonic microscopy reveals three domain variants related by 120$^\circ$ rotations, while ultrafast transient reflectivity measurements uncover a pronounced electronic reconstruction across the transition. Remarkably, the ordered phase appears only in lightly self-hole-doped compounds and is absent in insulating SmCd$_3$P$_3$, indicating an essential role for itinerant carriers despite their extremely low concentration. Guided by density functional theory, we develop a four-band model of the valence states and show that modest interactions can stabilize odd-parity electronic order. The resulting phase combines a spontaneous Fermi surface distortion with a momentum-dependent bilayer polarization that breaks inversion symmetry. Our results establish a route to interaction-driven parity breaking in dilute-carrier semiconductors and identify honeycomb bilayer systems as a promising platform for odd-parity electronic phases.

</details>


### [121] [Coexistence of static order and spin dynamics in an S = 5/2 frustrated triangular antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26921)
*U. Jena,B. Sana,Satish Kumar,M. Pregelj,A. Bandyopadhyay,P. Manuel,J. S. Lord,D. T. Adroja,P. Khuntia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过热力学、μSR、中子衍射实验和第一性原理计算，系统研究了三角晶格反铁磁体MnSnB₂O₆（Mn²⁺, S=5/2）的磁性质。该材料具有近乎完美的二维三角网络且无反位无序，在TN≈1 K处发生长程磁有序，比热在有序态下呈现C∝T¹·³⁷的非平凡幂律行为，零场μSR探测到与静态磁序共存的持续自旋动力学，中子衍射表明有序态符合3D Ising型反铁磁体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角晶格反铁磁体在经典高自旋极限下是研究竞争交换作用、各向异性和集体自由度之间相互作用的典范体系，可导致非传统低能激发、反常临界性和持续动力学响应。本文旨在通过实验与理论结合，深入理解这类阻挫磁体的新奇物态。

Method: 综合运用磁化率、比热进行热力学表征；零场μSR探测自旋动力学；中子衍射测定磁结构和序参量演化；第一性原理计算辅助解释交换相互作用。

Result: Curie-Weiss温度-12 K表明反铁磁主导；比热在TN≈1 K处显示长程磁有序，短程关联显著，有序态比热呈C∝T¹·³⁷的非平凡幂律；零场μSR确认50 mK以下存在磁有序，同时存在持续自旋动力学；中子衍射显示序参量演化符合3D Ising型反铁磁体。

Conclusion: MnSnB₂O₆是研究竞争交换相互作用和低能激发的理想阻挫磁体平台，其非平凡幂律行为和持续自旋动力学为理解阻挫体系中的奇异量子现象提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Frustrated triangular-lattice antiferromagnets in the classical high-spin limit provide a paradigmatic setting in which the interplay of competing exchange interactions, anisotropy, and collective degrees of freedom can lead to unconventional low-energy excitations, anomalous criticality, and persistent dynamical responses. Here, we present comprehensive thermodynamic, $μ$SR, and neutron diffraction experiments, along with first-principles calculations, on a triangular-lattice antiferromagnet, MnSnB$_2$O$_6$, where Mn$^{2+}$ ($S=5/2$) moments form a nearly perfect 2D triangular network without any anti-site disorder. The Curie-Weiss fit to the magnetic susceptibility yields a moderate Curie-Weiss temperature of $-12$ K, indicating dominant antiferromagnetic interactions between Mn$^{2+}$ moments, which is supported by first-principles calculations. Specific-heat measurements reveal the onset of long-range magnetic order at $T_{\rm N}\approx 1$ K, which is ascribed to intraplane exchange interactions. The specific heat exhibits pronounced short-range correlations above $T_{\rm N}$ and an unconventional power-law behavior, $C\propto T^{1.37}$, deep in the ordered state, suggesting the presence of non-trivial low-energy excitations. Zero-field $μ$SR experiments down to 50~mK confirm the presence of magnetic ordering below $T_{\rm N}$, in agreement with thermodynamic and neutron diffraction experiments. The $μ$SR measurements detect persistent spin dynamics coexisting with static magnetic order. The temperature evolution of the order parameter down to 50~mK from neutron diffraction suggests that the ordered state is consistent with a 3D Ising-like antiferromagnet. This family of archetypal frustrated magnets offers a promising venue for the experimental realization of emergent phenomena governed by competing exchange interactions and exotic low-energy excitations.

</details>


### [122] [Finite temperature precursors of Mottness in the Fermi Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27204)
*Sayantan Roy,Abhisek Samanta,Nandini Trivedi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文使用精确数值方法研究Hubbard模型在自旋有序温度以上的有限温度Mott物理前驱现象，发现Mottness首先通过双粒子响应中的电荷涨落抑制在反常金属态中出现，随后才体现在单粒子谱函数中；态密度带隙的形成源于布里渊区内的动量分辨谱权重重新分布，而非各动量点单粒子谱的带隙形成；掺杂后反常金属态产生与掺杂Mott绝缘体类似的输运和谱学特征，表明这些异常现象源于半满时前驱反常金属态中的强电荷响应重整化和谱权重重新分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Hubbard模型中Mott绝缘体形成的有限温度前驱现象及其物理机制，特别是理解Mottness如何在没有完全形成Mott绝缘体的情况下产生掺杂Mott绝缘体的特征行为。

Method: 使用精确行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法，在自旋有序温度以上对排斥Hubbard模型进行数值研究，分析单粒子和双粒子响应以及动量分辨谱权重分布。

Result: 发现Mottness首先通过双粒子响应（电荷涨落抑制）在反常金属态中显现，然后才体现于单粒子谱函数；态密度带隙是通过布里渊区内的动量分辨谱权重重新分布形成的；反常金属态在掺杂后产生掺杂Mott绝缘体的特征。

Conclusion: 掺杂Hubbard模型的输运和谱学异常不需要半满时完全形成的Mott绝缘体存在，而是源于半满时前驱反常金属态中发展出的强电荷响应重整化和谱权重重新分布。

Abstract: We investigate finite-temperature precursors of Mottness in the repulsive Hubbard model above the spin-ordering temperature, using numerically exact determinant quantum Monte Carlo. We show that the finite-temperature crossover is accompanied by a pronounced suppression of charge fluctuations, despite the presence of a gapless single-particle spectra, demonstrating that Mottness first emerges via two-particle response in an anomalous metallic regime, before appearing in single-particle spectral functions. We further show that a gap formation in the density of states occurs through momentum-resolved redistribution of spectral weight across the Brillouin zone, that begins at the onset of anomalous metallic regime, rather than through gap formation in single-particle spectral functions at individual momenta. Upon doping, the anomalous-metallic regime generates transport and spectroscopic signatures characteristic of doped Mott insulators. This shows that the transport and spectroscopic anomalies of the doped Hubbard model do not require the presence of a fully formed Mott insulator at half-filling, but instead originate from the strong charge-response renormalization and spectral-weight redistribution that develop within the precursor anomalous metallic regime at half-filling.

</details>


### [123] [Hidden-ordered Dirac fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27368)
*Bitan Roy*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种厄米扩展的洛伦兹对称狄拉克理论，通过引入另一个类质量反交换狄拉克算子，产生了隐序狄拉克费米子，其特征是任何维度下都具有线性能量-动量关系，并通过重整化费米速度表现出类似狄拉克系统的响应。这种隐序在d>1时将量子相变推向绝缘体，其存亡取决于候选绝缘序参量与类质量狄拉克算子之间的内部代数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展标准狄拉克理论，探索由隐藏序（hidden ordering）驱动的新型狄拉克费米子激发及其在量子临界点附近的行为。

Method: 通过将哈密顿量与另一个反交换的类质量狄拉克算子互补，构造厄米扩展的洛伦兹对称狄拉克理论；构建相应的晶格模型并分析其对称性保护与量子相变行为。

Result: 1）在任何维度下都保持线性能量-动量关系，产生隐序狄拉克费米子；2）隐序遍历重整化费米速度，响应类比于原始狄拉克系统；3）在d>1时，隐序将量子相变推向更强耦合的绝缘体；4）隐序在量子临界点附近的存亡取决于候选绝缘序参量与类质量狄拉克算子的内部代数关系。

Conclusion: 隐序狄拉克费米子是一种对称性保护的新型准粒子激发，可通过数值和实验平台（如所构建的晶格模型）进行验证。

Abstract: I propose a Hermitian extension of the Lorentz-symmetric Dirac theory by complementing the associated Hamiltonian with another \emph{masslike} anticommuting Dirac operator. The resulting theory manifests the iconic linear energy-momentum relationship in any dimension ($d$) and hence the emergent nodal quasiparticle excitations are named \emph{hidden-ordered Dirac fermions}, which are symmetry protected and their responses are analogous to those in original Dirac systems, however, in terms of a renormalized (due to the hidden ordering) Fermi velocity. Typically, such a hidden ordering pushes any quantum phase transition into an insulation toward even stronger coupling in any $d>1$. However, depending on the internal algebra between the candidate insulating order parameter and masslike Dirac operator, the hidden-ordering may survive or disappear near the corresponding itinerant quantum critical point. I construct lattice models for such hidden-ordered massless Dirac fermions and outline promising platforms (numerical and experimental) to test these predictions.

</details>

### [124] [Crime reduction through public healthcare: Interpretable machine learning for mental health service impacts in Greater London](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26202)
*Nadine Fässler,Ben Moews*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究利用伦敦街头的犯罪数据、心理健康转诊信息和社会经济指标，结合传统统计方法和机器学习技术，发现犯罪率与心理健康服务获取（以转诊率为代理指标）之间存在显著的正相关关系。与服务预防假说不同，研究揭示了一种U型关系：低水平服务具有预防效果，而高水平转诊则反映犯罪暴露后的需求响应。通过可解释人工智能和聚类分析，研究识别出四种自治市镇类型，各具不同的犯罪、心理健康服务和剥夺水平组合，表明需采取多层面而非一刀切的政策干预。这是针对英国公共资助医疗体系中该主题的首批综合性研究之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 犯罪、心理健康服务可及性与社会经济剥夺之间的关系在公共资助医疗体系中尚未得到充分探索，而该体系允许实施 impactful 的政策干预，为犯罪预防提供了替代视角。作者旨在填补这一关键研究空白。

Method: 研究使用伦敦大区的街道级犯罪数据、心理健康转诊信息和社会经济指标，同时采用传统统计方法和机器学习技术来识别相关关系和空间模式。后续分析使用可解释人工智能（XAI）和聚类分析来揭示不同犯罪类别的模式和自治市镇类型学。

Result: 发现犯罪率与心理健康转诊率（作为服务可及性的代理）之间存在持续的正相关关系。与简单的预防假说不同，存在一种U型关系：低服务水平有预防效果，高转诊率则反映犯罪暴露后的需求响应。聚类分析识别出四种自治市镇类型，各具独特的犯罪率、心理健康服务可及性和剥夺水平组合。

Conclusion: 研究为英国公共资助医疗体系中犯罪与心理健康服务的关系提供了首批全面证据，强调需要多层面的政策方法而非通用解决方案，并引入面向解释的分析方法以支持循证政策制定。

Abstract: The relationship between crime, mental health service access, and socioeconomic deprivation in publicly-funded healthcare systems allowing impactful policy interventions offers an alternative lens to crime prevention that remains underexplored. We address this critical gap through an analysis of street-level crime data, mental health referral information, and socioeconomic metrics across Greater London, using both traditional statistical methods and machine learning techniques to identify relevant relationships and spatial patterns to reveal a persistent positive association between crime rates and mental health referrals as a proxy for service access. The prevailing prevention hypothesis is contrasted with a nuanced U-shaped relationship suggesting a contrast between preventive effects at lower service levels and demand-driven responses to crime exposure for higher referral rates. Subsequent analyses, focussing on explainable artificial intelligence, show distinct crime category patterns, with a cluster analysis identifying four borough typologies with distinct combinations of crime rates, mental health service access, and deprivation levels, requiring multifaceted approaches rather than universal solutions. This research provides one of the first comprehensive studies on this topic for the UK's publicly-funded healthcare system and introduces interpretation-oriented approaches to uncover the patterns essential to evidence-based policies.

</details>


### [125] [False Positives, False Negatives, and the Detection-Only Problem: A Hierarchical Model for Species Occurrence with Observation Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26323)
*Kabiru Abubakari,Eleni Matechou,Marie C. Henniges,Ilia J. Leitch,Andrew R. Leitch,Silvia Liverani*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出将占有模型、物种分布模型和仅出现数据方法统一为一个层级观测过程框架，引入DN-T和DO两种数据类型，并为DO数据提出首个同时考虑假阳性和假阴性检测误差的层级模型，通过先验分布实现可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测物种出现对理解生物多样性变化、指导保护决策和评估环境压力对生态系统的影响至关重要，但现有统计方法（占有模型、物种分布模型、仅出现方法）之间的基本联系未被充分认识。

Method: 引入统一术语DN-T（T次访问的检测/未检测数据）和DO（仅检测数据），将现有模型视为层级观测过程的特例；针对DO数据提出层级模型，通过先验分布表达"物种更可能在出现地被记录"的信念来实现可识别性。

Result: 证明了DN-T（T>1）对应传统占有建模，DN-1对应物种分布建模，DO对应通常不准确地称为"仅出现数据"的方法；实现了DO模型的可识别性分析，并首次提出同时处理两类检测误差的层级模型。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架揭示了不同物种出现建模方法的内在联系，并为仅检测数据提供了更准确的统计处理方式，纠正了文献中对"仅出现数据"的术语使用。

Abstract: Monitoring species occurrence is essential for understanding biodiversity change, informing conservation decisions, and assessing the impact of environmental pressures on ecosystems. Species occurrence data arise from different survey designs, and the statistical literature has developed distinct corresponding modelling approaches, namely occupancy models, species distribution models, and presence-only methods, whose fundamental connections have remained largely unrecognised. We argue that these are all special cases of a single hierarchical observation process. To make these connections explicit, we introduce a unified terminology centred on two data types: detection/non-detection data with T visits (DN-T) and detection-only data (DO), where DN-T with T>1 corresponds to traditional occupancy modelling, DN-1 to species distribution modelling, and DO to what the literature commonly, but we argue inaccurately, calls presence-only data. Within this framework, we study the identifiability of DO models and propose a novel hierarchical model for DO data that, for the first time, explicitly accounts for both false positive and false negative detection errors. Identifiability is achieved through prior distributions that express the natural belief that a species is more likely to be recorded where it is present than where it is absent.
  ...

</details>


### [126] [Climate-Driven Mortality Forecasting Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26980)
*Kenrick So,Karim Barigou,Jens Robben*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段建模框架，将区域周度Lee-Carter基线模型与两种深度学习架构（CNN-LSTM和GNN-LSTM）相结合，用于捕捉极端气候导致的超额死亡率。两种架构均优于基线模型和MortFCNet，测试MSE降低约24%，尤其在老年群体中效果显著，并为保险公司和养老金基金提供了时变预测区间，以更准确地评估气候相关长寿风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统死亡率模型无法预测由气候极端事件引发的死亡率突增，存在建模空白。需要一种能够捕捉环境条件与气候冲击导致超额死亡率的建模框架。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段为区域周度Lee-Carter基线模型，捕捉长期趋势和季节性模式；第二阶段包括两种深度学习架构——CNN-LSTM（通过卷积滤波器捕捉区域特定时间响应）和GNN-LSTM（通过图表示建模空间死亡率依赖及气候影响跨区域传播）。两种架构均扩展为分位数LSTM框架以生成时变预测区间。

Result: 利用法国1990-2019年区域数据，两种模型均优于Lee-Carter基线和MortFCNet，测试MSE降低约24%，在老年群体（气候驱动死亡率峰值最严重）中提升尤为显著。模型成功捕捉了环境极端事件与超额死亡率之间的延迟和非线性关联。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了更现实的极端气候死亡率风险表征，时变预测区间为保险公司和养老金基金评估气候相关长寿风险提供了更可靠依据。

Abstract: Climate extremes have become important drivers of mortality, producing sudden spikes that traditional mortality models fail to predict. To address this gap, we propose a two-step modelling framework that combines a regional weekly Lee-Carter baseline model that captures long-term mortality trends and overall seasonal patterns, with two complementary deep learning architectures designed to model excess mortality driven by environmental conditions and climate shocks. The first, a CNN-LSTM, captures region-specific temporal responses through convolutional filters. The second, a GNN-LSTM, replaces convolutions with graph-based representations to model spatial mortality dependencies and the propagation of climate-related impacts across regions. Both architectures are further extended to a quantile LSTM framework that produces time-varying prediction intervals. We evaluate our models against both the Lee-Carter baseline and MortFCNet (Zheng et al., 2025). Using French regional data over 1990-2019, our models capture delayed and nonlinear associations between environmental extremes and excess mortality. Both proposed architectures outperform the Lee-Carter baseline and MortFCNet across all regions, each reducing test MSE by approximately 24% relative to the MortFCNet, with particularly large gains at the oldest ages where climate-driven mortality spikes are most severe. From a risk management perspective, the proposed framework provides a more realistic characterization of extreme climate-driven mortality risk, with time-varying prediction intervals that offer a more informed basis for the assessment of climate-related longevity exposure by insurers and pension funds.

</details>

### [127] [On Support Cardinality for Discrete Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26149)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究离散薛定谔方程非平凡解的最小可能支撑集大小，证明了维数约化原理：高维问题的最小支撑集不小于低维情形，并据此得到了四维空间中近乎最优的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索离散薛定谔方程非平凡解能有多稀疏，即解的非零格点数量（支撑集大小）的最小可能值。

Method: 通过建立维数约化原理：随着维数增加，最小可能支撑集大小不会减小。这使得低维下界可自动推广到高维。

Result: 得到了四维空间中近乎尖锐的下界，与已知最优的二维构造仅差一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 维数约化原理成立，低维下界可直接迁移至更高维，四维下界近乎最优。

Abstract: How sparse can a nontrivial solution of a discrete Schrödinger equation be? In this note we study Dirichlet solutions on a finite $d$-dimensional lattice box, allowing an arbitrary real potential, and measure sparsity by the number of lattice sites at which the solution is nonzero (assuming it is nonzero at the origin). Our main result is a dimension-reduction principle: the minimal possible support size cannot decrease when the dimension increases. Consequently, any lower bound proved in dimension $d-1$ automatically yields the same lower bound in dimension $d$. As an application, we obtain a nearly sharp lower bound in four dimensions, matching the best-known two-dimensional constructions up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [128] [The Colored Hofstadter Butterfly as a Many-Body Quantum Hall Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26256)
*Giovanna Marcelli,Tadahiro Miyao,Domenico Monaco,Stefan Teufel,Marius Wesle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于一大类弱相互作用晶格费米子系统，彩色Hofstadter蝴蝶具有多体解释。通过结合准绝热延拓与磁扰动估计，作者在三维参数空间中构建了存在局部唯一带隙基态的开区域，并在连通均匀带隙区域上证明了多体带隙标记定理：霍尔电导常数且量子化，满足2πσ^H∈Z。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在证明弱相互作用晶格费米子系统中彩色Hofstadter蝴蝶的多体对应物，验证非相互作用系统中出现的整数颜色（霍尔电导标签）在相互作用量子霍尔相中是否保持稳定。

Method: 将准绝热延拓（quasi-adiabatic continuation）与与分母无关的磁扰动估计（denominator-independent magnetic perturbation estimates）相结合，涵盖有/无有限磁单胞的周期和非周期磁通情况。

Result: 在三维参数空间(磁场b,化学势μ,相互作用强度λ)中构建了存在局部唯一带隙基态的开区域；证明了多体带隙标记定理：连通均匀带隙区域上霍尔电导常数且量子化，2πσ^H∈Z。

Conclusion: 非相互作用Hofstadter蝴蝶的整数颜色作为霍尔电导标签在多体相互作用下保持稳定，即相互作用量子霍尔相的拓扑不变性得以维持。

Abstract: We prove that the colored Hofstadter butterfly has a many-body interpretation for a broad class of weakly interacting lattice fermion systems. Starting from a spectral gap of a Hofstadter-like one-particle Hamiltonian at arbitrary magnetic flux $b$, we construct an open region in the three-dimensional parameter space $(b,μ,λ)$ of magnetic field, chemical potential, and interaction strength on which the infinite-volume interacting system has locally unique gapped ground states. The construction combines quasi-adiabatic continuation in the interaction strength with denominator-independent magnetic perturbation estimates, and therefore covers both commensurate and incommensurate fluxes, where no finite magnetic unit cell exists. On connected uniformly gapped regions meeting the non-interacting plane $λ=0$, we prove a many-body gap-labeling theorem: the Hall conductivity appearing in the macroscopic Ohm's law is constant and quantized, satisfying $2πσ^{\mathrm{H}}\in\mathbb{Z}$. Thus the integer colors of the non-interacting Hofstadter butterfly persist as Hall-conductivity labels of interacting quantum Hall phases.

</details>


### [129] [A new formulation of metriplectic dynamics with an application to quasigeostrophic ocean modeling with advected quantities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26388)
*Francisco J. Beron-Vera,Erwin Luesink*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的度量-辛动态（metriplectic dynamics）形式，通过两个反对称括号的乘积构造度量-辛四括号，并将其应用于二维准地转上层海洋模型，实现不可逆过程，确保能量守恒和熵增，灵活适用于从近物质守恒位涡到真实耗散的不同场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二维准地转（QG）海洋模型在处理包含平流量（如温度、盐度）的不可逆过程（如耗散、强迫）时缺乏统一的、热力学自洽的动力学形式。本文旨在建立一种通用的度量-辛框架，能够灵活引入不可逆性，同时严格满足热力学第一、第二定律。

Method: 通过将两个反对称括号相乘来构造度量-辛四括号（metriplectic four-bracket），从而得到通用的度量-辛动力学形式。将该形式应用于包含平流量的广义二维准地转上层海洋模型（以热QG模型为特例），通过特定的括号设计实现内能守恒和熵增，并允许根据需求调节不可逆过程的类型（从近守恒到真实耗散）。

Result: 成功构造了适用于含平流量二维QG模型的度量-辛动力学框架，该框架既能保证内能守恒和熵产生，又能灵活地描述从近位涡物质守恒到实际海洋中强迫与耗散的不同程度不可逆性。

Conclusion: 该度量-辛四括号形式为海洋动力学中不可逆过程的统一建模提供了新工具，在保证热力学一致性的前提下，具有灵活适配不同耗散强度的优势，对理解海洋环流中的热力学过程具有重要意义。

Abstract: A general formulation of metriplectic dynamics is presented, where the metriplectic four-bracket is constructed by multiplying two skew-symmetric brackets. The new formulation is then used to introduce irreversibility in a generalized two-dimensional (2D) quasigeostrophic (QG) upper-ocean model involving advected quantities, with the thermal QG model as a special case. By construction, the resulting dynamics ensure the conservation of internal energy and the generation of entropy, in accordance with the first and second laws of thermodynamics. Our metriplectic dynamics formulation allows for a flexible specification of irreversibility, ranging from a type that results in nearly material conservation of potential vorticity to the representation of realistic forcing and dissipation in 2D QG ocean modeling with advected quantities.

</details>


### [130] [Weak-Strong Resurgence Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26637)
*Gerald V. Dunne,Ayssar I. Farah*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了当弱耦合展开收敛半径为零而强耦合展开收敛半径为无穷大时，两者之间存在显式的重生对偶性，并通过Airy和Pearcey突变积分以及两个物理实例（零维φ⁴理论的Dyson-Schwinger方程、Gross-Neveu模型中的热核迹展开）加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完善重生对偶性理论：此前已知弱/强耦合展开收敛半径均为零或收敛半径有限情况下的对偶性，本文补充了零收敛半径与无穷大收敛半径之间的对偶情形。

Method: 利用Airy和Pearcey突变积分作为数学范例，通过重生（resurgence）分析建立弱耦合与强耦合展开之间的显式对偶关系，并将该框架应用于两个物理系统：零维φ⁴理论的Dyson-Schwinger方程（弱/强耦合展开）和Gross-Neveu模型中扭结-反扭结晶体的热核迹（短时/长时展开）。

Result: 成功揭示了收敛半径为零的展开与收敛半径为无穷大的展开之间的重生对偶性，并在突变积分和两个物理模型中验证了该对偶关系的成立。

Conclusion: 重生对偶性可推广至渐近展开收敛半径性质截然不同的情况，为弱耦合与强耦合微扰理论之间的深层联系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We show that there is an explicit resurgent duality between weak and strong coupling expansions when one of the expansions has zero radius of convergence and the other has infinite radius of convergence. This complements the situation where the convergent expansion has finite radius of convergence, or when both expansions have zero radius of convergence. We illustrate this phenomenon for the Airy and Pearcey catastrophe integrals, and we apply it to two physical examples: the weak and strong coupling expansions of Dyson-Schwinger equations in zero-dimensional scalar $φ^4$ theories, and the short and long time expansions of the heat kernel trace for the fluctuation operator of the kink-antikink crystal saddle configuration in the Gross-Neveu model.

</details>


### [131] [Wave-Particle Decomposition for Kinetic Equations I: Theory and Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26915)
*Chang Liu,Kun Xu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于局部演化时间尺度和动力学视界的波动-粒子分解（WPD）方法，将分布函数分解为解析累积的波动分量和纯动力学粒子分量，构建了跨Knudsen谱统一的波动-粒子系统，并通过保守的宏观-微观离散方法实现了多尺度高效数值模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一气体动力学波粒方法（UGKWP）采用全局时间步分裂，缺乏PDE层面的局部分解框架。本文旨在建立一种连续的、基于局部演化时间尺度与视界的波动-粒子分解方法，使分解在PDE层面定义而非算法层面，从而实现更自然的跨Knudsen谱统一描述。

Method: 基于特征积分解将分布函数分解为解析累积的波动分量和碰撞输运的粒子分量；构造包含无源守恒律、波动方程和粒子方程的统一系统；采用保守的宏观-微观离散方法，总守恒变量通过有限体积更新，波流通量采用Navier-Stokes气体动理论通量，粒子通量采用离散纵标（S_N）或蒙特卡洛方法计算。

Result: 形式分析证明了该方法的渐近保持连续极限、稀薄区域一致性以及主动动力学自由度的随域自适应缩放。一维、二维和三维数值测试验证了方法的精度、多尺度能力和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该波粒分解方法在PDE层面实现了基于局部演化时间尺度与松弛时间比值的分区，粒子分量由积分解的无碰撞因子生成的分数动力学种群控制，为多尺度动理学模拟提供了更统一高效的理论框架与数值方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a wave-particle decomposition (WPD) for kinetic relaxation equations, formulated around a local evolution timescale and its associated kinetic horizon. By leveraging the characteristic integral solution, we decompose the distribution function into an analytically accumulated wave component and a purely kinetic particle component. The latter is defined by the collisionless transport that survives beyond a prescribed local domain of influence, termed the horizon. This continuous formulation yields a unified wave-particle system valid across the entire Knudsen spectrum, comprising a source-free total conservation law, a wave equation, and a particle equation. The wave operator admits a Chapman--Enskog expansion, whose moments yield Euler and Navier--Stokes fluxes with horizon-dependent coefficients, while the particle equation governs the remaining non-equilibrium kinetic transport.
  At the algorithmic level, this system is discretized by a conservative macro-micro method. The total conservative variables are advanced by a finite-volume update using the sum of a Navier--Stokes gas-kinetic wave flux and a particle flux computed by either a deterministic discrete-ordinate $(S_N)$ method or a Monte Carlo representation. Unlike the global time-step splitting in the unified gas-kinetic wave-particle (UGKWP) method, the present partition is defined at the PDE level and governed by the local ratio of evolution timescale to relaxation time. The particle component is therefore a fractional kinetic population generated by the collisionless factor of the integral solution. Formal analysis establishes the asymptotic-preserving continuum limit, rarefied-regime consistency, and regime-adaptive scaling of active kinetic degrees of freedom. Numerical tests in one, two, and three dimensions validate the accuracy, multiscale capability, and efficiency of the framework.

</details>


### [132] [Asymptotic solutions: glancing trajectories, Lagrangian singularities, and Bessel cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26962)
*Ilya Bogaevskii,Michel Rouleux*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Hamilton-Jacobi方程解在相空间能量投影上的最简单Lagrangian奇点，并建立了其正规形。利用该正规形，近似求解了当$E_0$为$H|_{Λ_0}$的临界值时，半经典分布$f_h$微局部化在Lagrangian流形$Λ_0$上的方程$(\widehat{H}-E_0) \, u_h = f_h$的渐近解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理当能量$E_0$是Hamiltonian在Lagrangian流形$Λ_0$上的限制的临界值时，半经典分析中出现的奇点问题，特别是Lagrangian奇点在投影下的正规形理论。

Method: 通过寻找Hamilton-Jacobi方程解在空间-能量投影下的最简单Lagrangian奇点的正规形，并基于该正规形构造近似渐近解。

Result: 得到了Lagrangian奇点的正规形，并利用该正规形成功近似求解了临界值情形下的半经典方程。

Conclusion: 建立了临界值情形下半经典方程渐近解的理论框架，为处理更复杂的Lagrangian奇点提供了基础。

Abstract: We find a normal form for the simplest Lagrangian singularity appearing as the projection onto the space-energy of
  a solution to the Hamilton-Jacobi equation. Using this normal form we approximate asymptotic solutions of an equation
  $(\widehat{H}-E_0) \, u_h = f_h$ with a semiclassical distribution $f_h$ microlocalized on a Lagrangian manifold $Λ_0$
  when $E_0$ is a critical value of the restriction $H|_{Λ_0}$.

</details>


### [133] [The Ising Model Coupled to 2D Gravity: Critical Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27125)
*Maurice Duits,Nathan Hayford,Seung-Yeop Lee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在双标度极限下，具有四次相互作用的2-矩阵模型配分函数对数的微分收敛到(3,4)弦方程的τ函数微分，从而确认了随机曲面上的临界伊辛模型收敛到(3,4)拓扑极小模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Douglas-Shenker、Brézin-Kazakov以及Gross-Migdal等人提出的关于随机曲面上临界伊辛模型收敛到(3,4)拓扑极小模型的猜想。

Method: 基于双正交多项式族关联的Riemann-Hilbert问题的最速下降分析，并在局部参量构造中处理新的匹配问题。

Result: 严格证明了双标度极限下2-矩阵模型配分函数对数的微分与(3,4)弦方程τ函数微分的一致性。

Conclusion: 确认了随机曲面上的临界伊辛模型收敛到(3,4)拓扑极小模型这一理论结果，为矩阵模型与共形场论的联系提供了严格数学证明。

Abstract: We prove that the differential of the log of the partition function for the $2$-matrix model with quartic interactions converges in a certain double-scaling regime to the differential of the $\boldsymbolτ$-function for the $(3,4)$ string equation. This confirms the convergence of the critical Ising model on random surfaces to the $(3,4)$ topological minimal model, which was stated in the works of Douglas and Shenker, Brézin and Kazakov, and Gross and Migdal. Our analysis is based on a steepest-descent analysis of a Riemann-Hilbert problem associated to a family of biorthogonal polynomials. New features in the matching problem in the construction of local parametrices appear.

</details>


### [134] [Geometric bulk-edge correspondence for $\mathbb{Z}_2$-topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27318)
*Alexis Drouot,Jacob Shapiro,Xiaowen Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维时间反演不变绝缘体（Kitaev表中的AII类）在弯曲界面情况下的几何体-边对应关系：若两个这样的绝缘体被弯曲边界分隔在互补区域，则界面系统的Z2边缘指标等于两个体Z2指标差与边界及测量区域相关的几何相交数模2的乘积。这是此前对Hall绝缘体弯曲界面连接公式的Z2类比推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Z2拓扑绝缘体在弯曲界面下的体-边对应关系，将已有的Hall绝缘体弯曲界面结果推广到时间反演不变系统。

Method: 采用Z2版本的几何论证方法，类比DZ24中对Hall绝缘体的弯曲界面连接公式证明。

Result: 证明了弯曲界面下Z2边缘指标等于体Z2指标差与几何相交数模2的乘积。

Conclusion: 建立了AII类拓扑绝缘体的弯曲界面体-边对应关系，推广了Hall绝缘体的类似结果。

Abstract: Fermionic time-reversal-invariant insulators in two dimensions--class AII in the Kitaev table--come in two topological phases. These phases are characterized by a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-valued invariant, the Fu-Kane-Mele index. We prove a geometric bulk-edge correspondence for curved interfaces: if two such insulators occupy complementary regions separated by a curved boundary, then the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ edge index of the interface system is the product, modulo two, of the difference of the two bulk $\mathbb{Z}_2$ indices and a geometric intersection number associated with the boundary and the measurement region. The argument is a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ analogue of the curved-interface connection formula proved for Hall insulators in \cite{DZ24}.

</details>


### [135] [$\mathrm{W}^*$-algebraic Integration Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27366)
*Jan Głowacki,Yui Kuramochi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对W*-代数对(𝒮,ℛ)的算子值积分理论，定义了函数f: Σ→𝒮相对于POVM 𝖤的积分，其值位于空间张量积𝒮⊗̄ℛ中。通过商掉𝖤-零函数得到L^∞_𝖤(Σ,𝒮)空间，该空间在ℛ*可分时构成W*-代数。积分映射被证明是忠实正态完全正(CP)映射，在PVM情形为*-同态，在可局部化POVM情形为等距，并可通过Stinespring分解与Naimark扩张建立完全正性。最后给出了算子值莱布尼茨法则和Fubini定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典测度论中的积分概念推广到非交换（算子代数）框架下，使得函数可以取值于W*-代数𝒮，并相对于取值于另一W*-代数ℛ的POVM进行积分，从而统一处理量子信息、量子概率和算子理论中的积分构造。

Method: 利用空间张量积𝒮⊗̄ℛ构造积分值，定义一致有界超弱可测函数空间B_b(Σ,ℱ,𝒮)并通过𝖤-零函数构成商空间L^∞_𝖤(Σ,𝒮)；借助Stinespring分解和Naimark扩张证明完全正性；将积分映射分解为恒等映射与Φ_𝖤的空间张量积1_𝒮⊗̂Φ_𝖤。

Result: 建立了积分映射是忠实正态完全正映射；在PVM下是*-同态，在可局部化POVM下是等距；L^∞_𝖤(Σ,𝒮)在可分条件下同构于𝒮⊗̄L^∞_𝖤(Σ)且构成W*-代数；导出了算子值莱布尼茨法则和Fubini定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为算子值测度论提供了一个统一的积分框架，连接了经典测度、非交换概率与量子测量理论，并为后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Given a pair of $\mathrm{W}^*$-algebras $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})$ with $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})_*$ separable, a measurable space $(Σ, \mathcal{F})$ and a POVM $\mathsf{E}: \mathcal{F} \to \mathcal{E}(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})$, the integral of a function $f: Σ\to \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}$ is defined as an element of the spatial tensor product $\int f \otimes d\mathsf{E} \in \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R}$. The space $B_b(Σ,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})$ of uniformly bounded ultraweakly measurable functions is the universal domain of integration; once $\mathsf{E}$ is fixed it refines to the quotient $L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}) = B_b(Σ,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})/\mathcal{N}_\mathsf{E}$ by $\mathsf{E}$-null functions. When $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})_*$ is also separable, $L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}) \cong \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ)$ is a $\mathrm{W}^*$-algebra. The integration map is a faithful normal unital completely positive (CP) map, a $*$-homomorphism for PVMs and an isometry for localizable POVMs. It can be identified with the spatial tensor product $\boldsymbol{1}_{\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}} \hat{\otimes} Φ_\mathsf{E}$ where $Φ_\mathsf{E}: L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ) \to \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R}$ is the faithful normal positive map corresponding to $\mathsf{E}$. Complete positivity of integration maps is derived from Stinespring factorization through Naimark dilation. We establish an operator-valued Leibniz rule and Fubini theorem.

</details>

### [136] [A unified cell-merge algorithm for generating diverse Voronoi diagrams and new tessellations based on spatial chromatic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27235)
*Weining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间颜色模型的单元合并（CM）沃罗诺伊生成算法，该算法通过从统一数据库中检索和合并单元，能够快速生成多种类型沃罗诺伊图（如普通VD、球面VD、加权VD等），无需为每种VD开发特定算法，并可生成竞争强度图、耦合单元图等新型空间镶嵌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有沃罗诺伊图类型繁多（如普通VD、球面VD、线性实体VD、加权VD、高阶VD等），每种类型都需要开发特定的生成算法，缺乏一种统一的、能够快速生成多种VD的通用方法。

Method: 提出基于空间颜色模型的单元合并（Cell-Merge, CM）沃罗诺伊生成算法，通过从统一数据库中检索和合并单元来生成各种类型的VD，无需为每种VD开发专属算法。

Result: CM算法能够快速生成多种沃罗诺伊图变体，并能够生成竞争强度图和耦合单元图等经典VD无法生成的新型空间镶嵌。

Conclusion: 该算法适用于频繁需要多种VD进行计算和分析的场景（如基于位置的空间分析），提供了一种更高效、统一的VD生成方案。

Abstract: As a type of spatial tessellation model and an important spatial structure of computational geometry, Voronoi diagrams (VDs) are widely used in many fields. Due to differences in generation spaces, types of spatial entities, distance metrics, and relationships between entities and Voronoi regions, Voronoi diagrams vary into many types, such as the ordinary VD, VD on spheres, VD for linear entities, weighted VD, and ordered higher-order VD. These VDs also have their own generation algorithms. In this study, we propose a new cell-merge (CM) Voronoi generation algorithm based on the spatial chromatic model. The advantage of the CM algorithm is that it can quickly generate diverse VDs by retrieving and merging cells from a unified database, without requiring the development of specific algorithms for each VD. The CM Voronoi algorithm can be particularly applied in cases where a variety of Voronoi diagrams are frequently required for computation and analysis, such as in location-based spatial analysis. Furthermore, the proposed CM method can also generate some new types of spatial tessellations, such as competition intensity and couple-cell diagrams which are different from classical VDs.

</details>
