### [137] [Sensitivity of evolutionary entropy in Lefkovitch matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26170)
*Henrqiue M. Oliveira*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文发展了不可约Lefkovitch矩阵中进化熵的完整灵敏度理论，推导了生育率、转换率和保留率参数的显式闭式表达式，将Demetrius-Gundlach-Ziehe的熵灵敏度理论从年龄结构扩展到一般阶段结构种群，并提供了可用于比较人口统计分析、扰动研究、人口统计稳健性及生活史策略研究的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将进化熵的灵敏度理论从年龄结构的Leslie矩阵推广到一般阶段结构的Lefkovitch矩阵，填补阶段结构种群中熵扰动分析的理论空白。

Method: 利用Perron-Frobenius定理表示相关马尔可夫链，推导出平稳分布、世代时间、进化熵及其对生育率、转换率和保留率参数的偏导数的显式闭式表达式。

Result: 获得了熵的自然分解形式（转换与保留分量），阐明了阶段结构种群中人口不确定性的不同产生机制；该理论可立即特化为开群Leslie矩阵。

Conclusion: 将熵灵敏度理论从年龄结构扩展到一般阶段结构种群，为比较人口统计分析、扰动研究、人口统计稳健性及生活史策略研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Evolutionary entropy, introduced by Demetrius, is a demographic invariant that quantifies the temporal organization of structured populations. Explicit sensitivity expressions for this quantity were derived by Demetrius, Gundlach and Ziehe for age-structured Leslie matrices, establishing the foundations of entropy-based perturbation theory.
  In this paper we develop a complete sensitivity theory for evolutionary entropy in irreducible Lefkovitch matrices. Using the Perron--Frobenius representation of the associated Markov chain, we derive explicit closed-form expressions for the stationary distribution, generation time, evolutionary entropy and its partial derivatives with respect to fertility, transition and retention parameters. The resulting identities are expressed directly in terms of demographic coefficients, Perron eigenvectors, the dominant eigenvalue and the reproductive potential.
  The entropy representation obtained here gives a natural decomposition into transition and retention components and clarifies the distinct mechanisms through which demographic uncertainty is generated in stage-structured populations. We further show that the theory specializes immediately to open-group Leslie matrices, a class that has been shown to comprise a large fraction of empirical demographic models.
  The results extend the entropy sensitivity theory of Demetrius--Gundlach--Ziehe from age-structured to general stage-structured populations and provide practical tools for comparative demographic analysis, perturbation studies, demographic robustness, and the investigation of life-history strategies. Several biological examples are presented, illustrating how entropy decomposition and sensitivity analysis reveal complementary aspects of population organization.

</details>


### [138] [An Investigation of Additional Food Models with Generalised Functional Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26340)
*Urvashi Verma,Kanishka Goyal,Saptarshi Biswas,James I. Lathrop,Rana D. Parshad*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类广义的捕食者附加食物模型，建立了共存平衡态全局稳定的条件，发现了Holling IV型功能响应下的余维3 Bogdanov-Takens分岔，并通过确定性化学反应网络理论分析了附加食物引入导致的网络缺陷增加与复杂分岔之间的可能联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 附加食物常被用于增强捕食者对害虫种群的控制效果，但其模型的非对称结构导致动力学分析困难，因此需要建立更一般的分析框架来理解其稳定性与复杂动态行为。

Method: 首先建立广义附加食物模型的共存平衡态全局稳定条件；然后针对Holling IV型功能响应分析余维3的Bogdanov-Takens分岔；最后利用确定性化学反应网络理论（CRNT）研究模型的网络结构性质。

Result: 得到了共存平衡态全局稳定的条件；发现了Holling IV型功能响应下余维3的Bogdanov-Takens分岔；揭示了附加食物会增加反应网络缺陷度，并暗示高缺陷度与复杂分岔之间可能存在联系。

Conclusion: 附加食物的引入不仅影响捕食-被捕食系统的稳定性，还会提高系统的网络缺陷度，而高缺陷度可能与更复杂的动力学分岔行为相关联。这一发现为理解附加食物增强生物防治效果的机制提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Additional food sources are often used to improve the effectiveness of predators in controlling pest populations. However, the non-symmetric structure of additional food predator-prey models can cause certain aspects of their dynamics challenging to analyze. In this work, we study a general class of additional food models and establish conditions under which the coexistence equilibrium is globally stable. We then focus on a Holling type IV functional response with AF and show the existence of a Bogdanov-Takens bifurcation of codimension 3. We also study these models through the lens of deterministic chemical reaction network theory. Our analysis shows that the introduction of additional food increases the deficiency of the underlying reaction network and suggests a possible link between higher deficiency and complex bifurcations.

</details>


### [139] [Smoothly Time-Varying Continuous Time Markov Chains in Phylogenetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26402)
*Pratyusa Datta,Philippe Lemey,Marc A. Suchard*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种样条时钟模型（spline clock model），通过非齐次连续时间马尔可夫链（ICTMC）和三次B样条基展开建模时间变化的进化速率，利用高斯-勒让德数值积分近似似然计算，并采用高斯马尔可夫随机场先验约束平滑性。模拟和实证（泡沫病毒、SARS-CoV-2空间扩散）表明该模型能更准确恢复时变速率的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化速率估计对采样时间框架的依赖性构成了重建进化历史的根本挑战，现有分子钟模型难以灵活刻画速率随时间的变化。

Method: （1）使用非齐次连续时间马尔可夫链（ICTMC）沿系统发生树枝条建模序列替代过程；（2）将对数变换后的速率参数化为时间的三次B样条基展开的平滑函数；（3）采用高斯-勒让德数值积分高效近似分支上的速率函数积分；（4）通过高斯马尔可夫随机场先验约束样条系数的平滑性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，样条时钟模型比竞争模型更准确地恢复真实时变速率，且可信区间更窄；在泡沫病毒进化速率和SARS-CoV-2欧洲空间扩散速率两个实证数据中均检测到强时变信号。

Conclusion: 样条时钟模型是一种灵活、准确且能捕获时变进化速率信号的有效方法，为处理时间依赖性速率变化提供了新的统计框架。

Abstract: The dependence of evolutionary rate estimates on the timeframe of sampling poses a fundamental challenge for reconstructing evolutionary histories from molecular sequence data, which is central to evolutionary biology and infectious disease research. We present a novel and flexible approach to accommodate time-varying evolutionary rates by modeling the sequence substitution process using inhomogeneous continuous-time Markov chains (ICTMCs) acting along the branches of the phylogeny, and parameterizing the log transformed rate as a smooth function of time using a cubic B-spline basis expansion. Following the parlance of phylogenetics that refers to rates of molecular substitutions as molecular clocks, we call this a spline clock model. Integrals of the rate function over all branches, required for likelihood evaluation, are approximated efficiently using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and smoothness is enforced by assigning a Gaussian Markov random field prior to the spline coefficients. Through a simulation study, we demonstrate that the spline clock model recovers the true time-varying rates more accurately and with tighter credible intervals than competing clock models. We apply the spline clock model to examine the evolutionary rate of foamy virus and the rate of spatial diffusion of SARS-CoV-2 across Europe, recovering strong time-varying signal in both settings.

</details>


### [140] [Semialgebraic Conditions for Identifying Triangles in Phylogenetic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26673)
*Bryan Currie,Aviva K. Englander,Jose A. Esparza-Lozano,Elizabeth Gross,Max Hill,Colby Long,Devon Olds,Kawika O'Connor,Udani Ranasinghe,Christin Sum*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文针对带有Jukes-Cantor位点替换模型且无融合（coalescence）的三角嵌入系统发育网络，给出了三个三叶网络模型的完整半代数描述，证明尽管这些模型在代数上不可区分，但其交集和差集均为满维区域，因此网络参数不可识别（包括一般不可识别），并揭示了杂交信号随时间衰减直至三角边方向无法识别的生物学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育网络推断中，模型参数的可识别性是一个关键问题。先前研究表明，网络中三角形边的方向往往难以确定，甚至在某些模型下无法通过代数不变量（phylogenetic invariants）的标准代数技术来判定。本文旨在解决这一识别性难题，为三角嵌入网络的可识别性提供完整描述。

Method: 采用Jukes-Cantor位点替换模型（无融合过程），对三种包含嵌入三角形的三叶系统发育网络模型进行完整的半代数（semialgebraic）描述。通过分析这些基例（base cases）模型的代数几何性质，研究其交集和差集的维度特征。

Result: 1）给出了三个三叶Jukes-Cantor网络模型的完整半代数描述；2）证明任意两个模型的交集和差集在点位概率分布空间中均为满维区域（full-dimensional regions）；3）表明这些网络模型虽在代数上不可区分，但并不等同，且不可识别（或一般不可识别）。

Conclusion: 杂交事件产生的信号在发生时可立即检测，但随时间推移逐渐衰减，最终导致网络三角形中边的方向无法识别。这为系统发育网络中三角结构的参数不可识别性提供了清晰的生物学解释。

Abstract: An important consideration for a model-based method of phylogenetic network inference is the identifiability of the network parameter of the model. A recurring theme in previous works exploring this issue is that it is often difficult to identify the orientation of edges in a triangle of the network. In fact, it has been shown that for some models it is impossible to determine the orientation of triangle edges utilizing the standard algebraic technique of phylogenetic invariants. In this work, we consider one such model with a Jukes-Cantor site-substitution process and no coalescence. We give a complete semialgebraic description of three, 3-leaf Jukes-Cantor phylogenetic network models with embedded triangles. By describing these base cases, we resolve several questions about the identifiability of networks with embedded triangles. We show that for any pair of models, the intersection and set differences of the models are full-dimensional regions of the space of site-pattern probability distributions. Thus, despite being algebraically indistinguishable, these network models are not identical, nor are they identifiable (or generically identifiable). Our results also yield a straightforward biological interpretation--that the signal from a hybridization event may be immediately detectable but decays over time until it is impossible to identify the orientation of edges in the triangle of a network.

</details>


### [141] [On-farm management strategies for reducing H5N1 transmission in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26674)
*Oliver Eales,Scott Ison,Rachael Gibney,Zoe Vogels,James M. McCaw,Freya M. Shearer*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过数学模型分析H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b在奶牛场的传播动力学，发现将奶牛分为"挤奶队列"（分群饲养并按固定顺序挤奶）能有效降低暴发风险，同时证明频繁的散装奶样检测（如每周一次）可快速发现疫情并启动干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b自2024年初传入美国奶牛场并引发暴发，对动物健康、奶牛产业经济和人类健康构成重大威胁，但不同传播途径的相对贡献尚不明确，亟需评估和制定防控策略。

Method: 建立奶牛场H5N1传播动力学的数学模型，考虑多种可能的传播途径，分析影响暴发风险的关键因素，并评估"挤奶队列"分组管理和散装奶样检测等干预措施的效果。

Result: 将奶牛分为"挤奶队列"（分群饲养、固定顺序挤奶）在无论何种主导传播途径下均能高效降低暴发风险；该策略在暴发前预防性实施效果最佳，且新引入牛只应归入最后挤奶的队列；每周一次散装奶样检测可快速发现疫情并支持及时实施反应性干预。

Conclusion: 研究结果为制定H5N1奶牛场暴发应对的管理指南提供了科学依据，支持通过队列管理和定期监测来有效控制疫情。

Abstract: Introductions of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b into dairy cattle have resulted in outbreaks on dairy farms across the United States since early-2024. Outbreaks have significant consequences for animal health, result in economic losses for the dairy industry, and pose a threat to human health. Though the relative contributions of different on-farm transmission pathways remain a key uncertainty, a major route is considered to be through repeated contamination of milking stalls (i.e. the equipment and area where an individual cow is milked) due to the milking of infected animals. Here we develop mathematical models of H5N1 transmission dynamics on dairy farms, considering multiple possible transmission pathways, and identify factors that contribute to outbreak risk and on-farm interventions for mitigating risk. In particular, we demonstrate that dividing cattle into 'milking cohorts', with cohorts kept in separate pens or paddocks and milked in the same order every day, would be highly effective at mitigating outbreaks irrespective of the dominant transmission pathway. Cohorting cattle is most effective when implemented pre-emptively (i.e. before an outbreak) and when newly introduced cattle are kept in the final milking cohort. Additionally, we demonstrate that frequent bulk milk sample testing (e.g. weekly) would enable the rapid detection of outbreaks and implementation of reactive interventions (or scaling up of existing interventions). Our findings can support the development of management guidelines for effectively responding to H5N1 outbreaks in dairy cattle.

</details>


### [142] [Hyperiax and Phylogenetic Inference from Shape Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26974)
*Gefan Yang,Marcus Teller,Christy Hipsley,Rasmus Nielsen,Stefan Sommer*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Hyperiax，一个基于JAX的开源库，用于高效执行树遍历算法和消息传递，特别支持BFFG框架，实现对高维形态性状（如蝶翅和鸟喙3D地标数据）在大规模系统发育树上的参数推断和祖先状态重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维形态性状的系统发育推断需要同时处理形状数据的非线性几何和系统发育树结构，但现有的BFFG框架因缺乏高效实现而限制了实际应用。

Method: 开发了基于JAX的开源库Hyperiax，支持树上消息传递和高效树遍历算法，结合BFFG框架，支持离散时间和随机微分方程模型的推断。

Result: 在850节点和696节点的大型系统发育树上，分别对118个二维地标（蝶翅）和79个三维地标（鸟喙）实现了参数推断和祖先状态重建，规模和分辨率均超越此前可行范围。

Conclusion: Hyperiax通过高效实现BFFG框架，显著扩展了高维形态性状系统发育推断的可行规模，为进化生物学中非线性形状数据分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Phylogenetic inference on high-dimensional morphological traits requires algorithms that account for both the nonlinear geometry of the shape data and the phylogenetic tree structure. The Backward Filtering Forward Guiding (BFFG) framework provides smoothing for nonlinear stochastic processes on trees and enables inference of parameters and ancestral states. As practical adoption has been limited by a lack of efficient implementations, we present Hyperiax, an open-source library for tree traversal algorithms and message passing using JAX, designed particularly to support operations needed for BFFG. Hyperiax enables efficient execution of operations on trees with large numbers of nodes and, coupled with the BFFG-specific operations, this allows efficient inference in both discrete-time and stochastic differential equation models. Concretely, we demonstrate that Hyperiax enables parameter inference and ancestral reconstruction for butterfly wing shapes represented by landmarks in two dimensions, and analyses of avian beaks from landmarks in three dimensions. Both cases demonstrate application of BFFG on two substantially larger phylogenetic trees with 850 and 696 nodes with higher resolution shape data (118 two-dimensional landmarks and 79 three-dimensional landmarks, specifically) than previously possible.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Higher-Order vs. Quadratic Binary Optimization: Which Is Better for Probability Optimization with Tensor Sampling?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26206)
*Hadi Salloum,Kirill Novoselov,Aleksandr Pochtarev,Yaroslav Kholodov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了HUBO和QUBO模型在概率优化中的优劣势，发现当问题包含高阶交互时，原生HUBO公式更优，而QUBO在天然二次问题上仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索HUBO和QUBO模型在概率优化中的比较优势，为实际问题中选择合适的优化框架提供指导。

Method: 结合理论分析（公式表达能力和降阶开销）与实验验证（合成多项式目标、RSA分解、Max-Cut实例），使用张量采样技术进行概率优化。

Result: 原生HUBO在处理高阶交互问题时更优，因为将其降阶为QUBO会增加张量采样器看到的有效维度；QUBO在天然二次问题上仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 应在模型保真度、维度增长和求解器性能之间进行平衡，为实际选择优化框架提供了实践指导。

Abstract: This article explores the comparative strengths of Higher-Order Unconstrained Binary Optimization (HUBO) and Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) models in the context of probability optimization using tensor sampling techniques. HUBO can represent interactions beyond pairwise terms without introducing auxiliary variables, while QUBO benefits from a simpler algebraic structure and a wide range of established solvers. We combine a theoretical analysis of formulation expressiveness and reduction overhead with empirical experiments on synthetic polynomial objectives, RSA factorization, and Max-Cut instances. The results show that native HUBO formulations are often preferable when the original problem contains high-order interactions, because reducing such problems to QUBO increases the effective dimension seen by tensor samplers. At the same time, QUBO remains competitive for naturally quadratic problems. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting an optimization framework that balances model fidelity, dimension growth, and solver performance.

</details>


### [144] [Three-Objective Integral R2 Subset Selection: NP-Hardness and Submodular Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26591)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究三目标优化中基于积分R2指标的代表点选择问题：证明了该问题的子模性（保证(1-1/e)近似比），同时证明精确子集选择是NP-hard的，从而将可处理的二目标情况与三目标情况区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从有限帕累托前沿近似中选择固定数量的代表点是多目标优化中的基本后处理任务。本文针对三目标情况下的积分R2指标研究该问题，旨在理解其计算复杂度并设计可理论保证的近似算法。

Method: 1) 证明积分R2改进量是单调子模集函数，基于理想点的R2指标可通过贪心选择获得(1-1/e)的间隙缩减保证；2) 给出基于细分法的贪心实现，最坏情况时间复杂度O(n^6)；3) 通过视角变换将切比雪夫阴影改进映射为加权锚框并问题，并改编三维锚框构造，证明精确子集选择是NP-hard的。

Result: 正面结果：积分R2改进量具有单调子模性，贪心算法至少能闭合最大可能R2间隙的(1-1/e)比例；负面结果：三目标情况下精确固定基数子集选择是NP-hard的。

Conclusion: 这些结果将可处理的二目标情况与三目标情况区分开来，同时识别了一条基于子模优化的有理论保证的近似路径。

Abstract: Selecting a fixed number of representative points from a finite Pareto-front approximation is a fundamental post-processing task in multiobjective optimization. This paper studies this problem for the integral R2 indicator in three objectives, where the indicator is defined as the integral of the lower envelope of weighted Tchebycheff scalarizations over the two-dimensional weight simplex. We provide two complementary algorithmic results. On the positive side, we show that the integral R2 improvement with respect to any fixed baseline is a monotone submodular set function. For the usual ideal-point based R2 indicator, with the ideal point fixed, this yields a direct gap-reduction guarantee: greedy selection closes at least a $(1-1/e)$-fraction of the maximum possible R2 gap between a fixed dominated anchor value and the best cardinality-$k$ value. We also give a tested greedy implementation that evaluates exact integral R2 values by subdivision, with worst-case running time $O(n^6)$. On the negative side, we prove that exact fixed-cardinality subset selection is NP-hard already in three objectives. The hardness proof uses a perspective transformation that maps Tchebycheff-shadow improvements to a weighted anchored-box union problem with density $(x_1+x_2+x_3)^{-4}$, and then adapts the three-dimensional anchored-box construction of Bringmann, Cabello, and Emmerich. Together, these results separate the tractable two-objective case from the three-objective case while identifying a principled approximation route based on submodular optimization.

</details>


### [145] [Input Convex Neural Network as a Surrogate in Stability-Constrained Optimization for IBR-dominated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26446)
*Wangkun Xu,Hongyang Jia,Yi Wang,Ning Zhang,Fei Teng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文指出了输入凸神经网络（ICNN）作为电力系统优化约束时的两个常见公式化局限：1）使用通用Big-M混合整数重构会引入不必要的辅助二进制变量；2）反转稳定不等式会将凸子水平集变为非凸超水平集，破坏基于割线方法的全局收敛保证。针对这些问题，提出了基于LP的精确epigraph重构（ReLU-ICNN）、具有全局保证的外逼近方案（子水平集约定）以及保持可行性的内逼近方案（超水平集约定），并在IEEE 14节点和118节点机组组合实例中进行了仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在使用ICNN作为电力系统稳定性指标的替代模型并嵌入优化约束时，存在两个未被充分认识的公式化局限，这些局限会抵消ICNN的凸性优势，导致计算效率降低甚至收敛性失效。

Method: 1）针对ReLU-ICNN提出精确的线性规划（LP）基epigraph重构；2）在子水平集约定下提出具有全局收敛保证的外逼近方案；3）在超水平集约定下提出保持可行性的内逼近方案，并在IEEE 14节点和118节点系统上进行仿真验证。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点和118节点机组组合实例上的仿真结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: ICNN在电力系统优化中具有重要应用价值，但必须注意正确的公式化约定：子水平集方向能保持凸性并享有全局收敛保证，而超水平集方向则会破坏凸性结构；本文提供的重构和逼近方案可有效规避这些局限。

Abstract: Input convex neural networks (ICNNs) are increasingly used as surrogates for stability indices and embedded as constraints in power-system optimization. This letter clarifies two recurring formulation limitations that can negate ICNN convexity benefits: (i) applying generic Big-$M$ mixed-integer reformulations introduces auxiliary binaries that are unnecessary for enforcing ICNN sublevel constraints; and (ii) reversing the stability inequality transforms a convex sublevel set into a generally nonconvex superlevel set, invalidating global-convergence guarantees of cut-based methods. After clarifying the limitations, we provide (i) an exact LP-based epigraph reformulation for ReLU-ICNNs, (ii) an outer-approximation scheme with global guarantees under the sublevel convention, and (iii) a feasibility-preserving inner-approximation scheme for the superlevel convention, with simulations on IEEE 14- and 118-bus unit commitment instances.

</details>


### [146] [Minkowski-Type Wasserstein Metrics and Barycenters for Location-Scale Mixtures with Application to Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26482)
*Songyan Luo,Yunxin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有限位置-尺度混合模型的离散最优传输框架，通过将连续多边缘OT问题简化为混合组件间的离散传输问题，使计算复杂度从二次降至线性，并在VisDA-C基准上验证了其高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散OT在经验测度间进行逐点匹配，计算成本随样本量至少呈二次增长，亟需更高效的替代方案。

Method: 基于广义Minkowski不等式定义特定函数类，刻画多变量位置-尺度族间的OT映射；在可识别性假设下，将Wasserstein度量与重心拓展至混合模型；通过限制联合耦合为特定混合结构，将连续多边缘OT问题转化为混合组件间的离散运输问题。

Result: 计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)；在VisDA-C基准上取得与经验OT方法相当的精度，同时大幅降低计算开销与内存占用。

Conclusion: 所提混合模型OT框架在保持竞争力的同时实现了线性计算复杂度，为大规模OT应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Discrete optimal transport (OT) typically relies on pointwise matching between empirical measures, incurring computational costs that scale at least quadratically with the sample size. To circumvent this limitation, we introduce a mathematical framework for OT between finite location-scale mixture models. By defining a specific function class grounded in generalized Minkowski inequalities and characterizing OT maps between multivariate location-scale families, we extend Wasserstein-type metrics and barycenters to these mixture models under the assumption of identifiability. Furthermore, we prove that restricting joint couplings to a specific mixture structure reduces the continuous multimarginal OT problem to a discrete transport problem over mixture components. Computing transport plans between these components rather than individual samples reduces the computational complexity to linear scaling with respect to the sample size. Empirical evaluations on the VisDA-C benchmark confirm that this strategy achieves competitive accuracy compared to existing empirical OT approaches, while substantially reducing the computational cost and memory footprint.

</details>


### [147] [Mean-Field PhiBE: Continuous-Time Mean-Field Reinforcement Learning from Discrete-Time Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26498)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Martin Hernandez,Qinxin Yan,Yuhua Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于离散时间数据的连续时间平均场控制问题，提出了MF-PhiBE方法，将离散时间转移信息融入Wasserstein空间上的连续时间PDE，并构建了无模型演员-评论家算法，理论上证明了O(Δt)的一阶精度，在LQ情形下可达二阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型未知且仅能获取离散时间转移数据的条件下，连续时间平均场控制面临两难：基于模型的HJB方程需要漂移和扩散系数，但离散数据无法辨识这些系数；直接离散化Bellman方程虽可规避非可辨识性问题，却丢失了微分方程结构。因此需要一种方法桥接两者，既利用离散数据又保留连续时间PDE结构。

Method: 提出Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE)，在Wasserstein空间上的连续时间PDE中嵌入离散时间转移信息：用数据驱动的单步估计器替代未知的无穷小漂移和协方差，同时保留McKean-Vlasov HJB方程的生成元结构；推导了熵正则化随机反馈策略的策略梯度定理，通过逐动作无穷小优势和策略得分表达演员方向；结合两者构建无模型演员-评论家算法。

Result: 理论上证明MF-PhiBE最优策略诱导的值函数逼近连续时间最优值，误差阶为O(Δt)；在线性二次型情形下，仅需单步数据即可达到二阶精度；在LQR基准和人群厌恶问题上的数值实验验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: MF-PhiBE成功弥合了离散数据与连续时间PDE之间的鸿沟，提供了一种可证明收敛的无模型平均场控制方法，且在实际问题中表现良好。

Abstract: This paper addresses model-free continuous-time mean-field control in a setting where the population dynamics evolve continuously according to an unknown McKean-Vlasov stochastic differential equation, while only discrete-time transition data are available. In the model-based formulation, policy evaluation is naturally described by a stationary Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation on $\mathcal P_2(\mathbb R^d)$, but this equation involves the drift and diffusion coefficients of the controlled McKean-Vlasov dynamics, which are not identifiable when only discrete-time data are available. On the other hand, a direct reduction to a time-discrete Bellman equation avoids the non-identifiability issue but loses the differential equation structure. To bridge these two viewpoints, we introduce a Mean-Field-PhiBE (MF-PhiBE), which incorporates discrete-time transition information into a continuous-time PDE on the Wasserstein space. The MF-PhiBE replaces the unknown infinitesimal drift and covariance in the policy-evaluation equation by one-step estimators computed from data, while preserving the generator structure of the McKean-Vlasov HJB equation. We also derive a policy-gradient theorem for entropy-regularized randomized feedback policies, expressing the actor direction through an action-wise infinitesimal advantage and the score of the policy. Combining these two ingredients yields a model-free actor-critic method. We prove a first-order consistency estimate showing that the value induced by an optimal MF-PhiBE policy approximates the optimal continuous-time value with an error of order $Δt$. In the linear-quadratic case, we show our approximation achieves second-order accuracy with only one-step data. Numerical experiments on an LQR benchmark and a crowd-aversion problem illustrate the proposed framework.

</details>


### [148] [Maximizing Throughput in an M/G/1 Queue with Customer Abandonments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26544)
*Runhua Wu,Hayriye Ayhan,Douglas G. Down*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带顾客放弃的单服务器队列中最优服务分配策略。证明当服务时间服从Erlang-K或超指数分布且决策者能观察服务阶段时，最短剩余期望处理时间（SREPT）策略能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且与放弃率无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带顾客放弃的队列系统中，如何分配服务资源以最大化吞吐量是一个关键问题。特别是在服务时间具有阶段结构时，需要研究最优的服务策略。

Method: 考虑泊松到达、指数分布耐心时间、Erlang-K或超指数服务时间的单服务器队列系统。假设决策者可观察顾客的服务阶段，分析SREPT策略的吞吐量最优性。

Result: 证明了在Erlang-K和超指数服务时间分布下，SREPT策略能最大化长期平均吞吐量，且该最优性对所有放弃率都成立。

Conclusion: SREPT策略在带顾客放弃的单服务器队列中是最优的，其最优性不依赖于具体的放弃率参数。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of identifying the optimal server assignment policy in single-server queues with customer abandonment. We consider a system with Poisson arrivals and exponentially distributed patience times. We show that when service times follow either an Erlang-$K$ or a hyperexponential distribution and the decision maker can observe the phase of a customer's service time, the Shortest Remaining Expected Processing Time (SREPT) policy maximizes the long-run average throughput, independent of the abandonment rate.

</details>


### [149] [The parental parsimony problem on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26919)
*Martin Frohn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对系统发育网络中的亲本简约得分问题（PPS），首次提出了在任意但固定叶标签下的常数因子近似算法，并引入了一种精确求解算法，通过模拟数据验证了其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育重建是计算生物学的主要挑战之一。在现有系统发育网络重建方法中，一个重要子任务是：在扩展网络叶标签的基础上，确定网络内最简约的树。该子任务存在不同变体，取决于网络所捕获的不同进化现象（如杂交、重组、异源多倍体化或不完全谱系分选）。当这些现象同时存在时，求解最简约树即成为PPS问题，这是一个NP-hard的组合优化问题，亟需有效的近似与精确算法。

Method: 针对PPS问题，本文提出了首个常数因子近似算法（适用于任意但固定的叶标签及一类NP-hard网络，即二元、半单纯、树子代系统发育网络）；同时提出了一个针对二元、树子代系统发育网络的精确求解算法，并在模拟数据上进行了性能分析。

Result: 首次给出了PPS问题的常数因子近似保证；引入了一种新的精确求解算法，并通过模拟实验分析了其性能表现。

Conclusion: 本文为PPS问题提供了理论上的近似保证及实用的精确求解方法，填补了该领域在近似算法方面的空白，并为后续系统发育网络分析中的最简约树求解奠定了基础。

Abstract: Phylogenetic reconstruction is one of the major challenges in computational biology. Among existing reconstruction methods for phylogenetic networks, an important subtask emerges in extending a leaf-labelling on a phylogenetic network to determine a most parsimonious tree inside the network. There exist different variants of this subtask depending on the biological model assumptions for which distinct evolutionary phenomena are captured by the network. In this article we assume that next to hybridization or recombination events, also allopolyploidy or incomplete lineage sorting are present. Then, finding the most parsimonious tree inside the network is called the parental parsimony score problem (PPS), a NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. We provide the first constant-factor approximation for the PPS on arbitrary but fixed leaf labels and a class of networks on which the PPS remains NP-hard, namely binary, semi-simplex, tree-child phylogenetic networks. Furthermore, we introduce a novel exact solution algorithm for the PPS on binary, tree-child phylogenetic networks and analyze its performance on simulated data.

</details>


### [150] [Comparative Analysis of Linear Battery Models for Carbon Emission Optimization in Solar Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26648)
*Anna Sacilotto,Ambrogio Maria Bernardelli,Stefano Gualandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究住宅光伏-电池储能系统（PV-BESS）在不确定性下最小化等效碳排放的问题，开发并比较了从规则自耗启发式到全随机线性反馈控制的一系列优化模型。实验表明，直接随机优化（程序化电池模型）显著优于启发式控制，接近理论下界，而基于反馈的模型泛化能力有限且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在住宅光伏-电池储能系统中，如何在不确性（负荷、太阳能发电、电网碳强度随机波动）下有效最小化等效碳排放，目前缺乏系统性的优化框架对比研究。

Method: 构建从规则自耗启发式到全随机线性反馈控制的递阶线性优化模型族；通过主成分分析生成大规模场景集以刻画随机性；采用随机规划方法优化期望碳排放。

Result: 程序化电池模型（直接随机优化）大幅优于启发式控制，碳排放接近全知基准的理论下界；反馈模型在训练集上略有提升但泛化不佳，且计算成本更高。

Conclusion: 线性随机规划为分布式PV-BESS系统的碳排放管理提供了有效且易处理的框架，无需复杂反馈控制即可接近最优性能。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of minimizing equivalent carbon emissions in residential photovoltaic-battery energy storage systems (PV-BESS) under uncertainty. We develop and compare a hierarchy of linear optimization models that differ in their degree of anticipativity and feedback complexity, ranging from a rule-based self-consumption heuristic to fully stochastic formulations with linear feedback control. The proposed models explicitly incorporate the stochastic variability of household load, solar production, and grid carbon intensity through large scenario sets generated via principal component analysis of real operational data. Computational experiments on synthetic yet realistic scenarios show that direct stochastic optimization of expected emissions (Programmed Battery model) substantially outperforms heuristic control, achieving emission reductions close to the theoretical lower bound provided by the Omniscient Battery benchmark. Feedback-based models marginally improve training performance but do not generalize better on unseen data, while incurring higher computational costs. Overall, results demonstrate that linear stochastic programming provides an effective and tractable framework for emission-aware energy management in distributed PV-BESS systems.

</details>


### [151] [Sinkhorn-Knopp balancing with generalised martingale-type constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26739)
*Abigail Langbridge,Martin Corless,Robert Shorten*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Sinkhorn算法的熵正则化最优传输框架，用于解决存在一般运输约束和异构柔性边际约束的资源分配问题，并给出了收敛性证明和实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中（如共享经济、鞅问题），不仅需要最优运输计划，还需要考虑其实现过程，而经典最优传输模型难以处理包含一般运输约束和异构柔性边际约束的复杂资源分配问题。

Method: 将经典熵正则化最优传输公式进行推广，设计可证明收敛的Sinkhorn类算法来求解包含一般运输约束和异构柔性边际约束的资源分配问题。

Result: 提出了收敛性有保证的Sinkhorn-like算法，并通过实例验证了所提方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的推广熵正则化最优传输框架及其对应的Sinkhorn算法能够有效处理包含一般运输约束和异构柔性边际约束的资源分配问题，在理论和实践上都具有良好表现。

Abstract: We consider the problem of optimally distributing resources from a set of suppliers to a set of consumers in the presence of general transportation constraints, and including heterogeneous flexibility in the marginal constraints. Such problems frequently arise in a variety of practical settings; for example, in the context of sharing economy applications, where one is not only interested in the transportation plan, but also its realisation, and in other problems that involve the study of martingales. Our principal contribution in this paper is to consider a generalisation of the classic entropically regularised Optimal Transport formulation in which such problems can be solved with a Sinkhorn algorithm. In particular, we present provably convergent Sinkhorn-like algorithms for solving this class of problems, and provide examples to both illustrate the utility of our approach as well as its efficacy.

</details>


### [152] [Second-order Methods for Multiobjective Composite Optimization: Preconditioning Strategies, Subspace Variants and Inexact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26792)
*Jian Chen,Xinmin Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对多目标复合优化问题中光滑分量病态时数值求解的挑战，提出了一种预处理近端Barzilai-Borwein方法。该方法结合目标-wise BB缩放与公共预条件子，通过二维子空间变体避免非对角度量近端映射，并扩展至包含线性算子形式的非光滑项。在非凸设定下证明了全局收敛性，并在误差界条件下证明了线性收敛率。数值实验验证了其在病态ℓ1正则化、结构化ℓ1正则化及线性约束问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标复合优化问题广泛存在于稀疏正则化、约束多目标模型和多任务学习中，但当光滑分量病态时，现有方法存在瓶颈：近端梯度法迭代简单但收敛慢，近端牛顿/拟牛顿法利用曲率信息但需计算昂贵的度量近端映射。亟需一种既利用曲率信息又避免高计算开销的方法。

Method: 1) 提出预处理近端Barzilai-Borwein方法，结合目标-wise BB缩放（减少目标间失衡）与公共预条件子（捕获共享曲率信息）。2) 开发子空间变体，在由近端梯度型方向和投影历史方向生成的二维子空间中计算搜索方向，通过构建共轭基将子空间模型分解为可解的一维子问题，避免非对角度量近端映射。3) 扩展至非光滑项g_i(Ax)形式，通过线性算子感知预条件子，利用对偶子问题实现显式近端评估。4) 分析基于松弛下降条件的非精确版本。

Result: 在非凸设定下建立了非精确算法的全局收敛性，在误差界条件下证明了线性收敛率。在病态ℓ1正则化、结构化ℓ1正则化及线性约束问题上的数值实验表明该方法有效。

Conclusion: 提出的预处理近端Barzilai-Borwein方法有效解决了多目标复合优化中病态问题的数值求解挑战，兼顾了曲率信息利用与计算效率，并具有良好的理论保证和实证表现。

Abstract: Multiobjective composite optimization problems arise in sparse regularization, constrained multiobjective models, and multi-task learning, but their numerical solution remains challenging when the smooth components are ill-conditioned. Proximal gradient methods are inexpensive per iteration but may converge slowly, while proximal Newton and quasi-Newton methods exploit curvature information at the cost of evaluating expensive metric proximal mappings. To address these issues, we propose a preconditioned proximal Barzilai--Borwein method for multiobjective composite optimization. The method combines objective-wise Barzilai--Borwein scaling, which reduces imbalance among objectives, with a common preconditioner that captures shared curvature information. To avoid non-diagonal metric proximal mappings, we develop a subspace variant in which the search direction is computed in a two-dimensional subspace generated by a proximal-gradient-type direction and a projected historical direction. By constructing a conjugate basis with respect to the preconditioning metric, the subspace model decomposes into tractable one-dimensional subproblems. The framework is further extended to nonsmooth terms of the form $g_i(Ax)$ through a linear-operator-aware preconditioner, yielding explicit proximal evaluations via dual subproblems. We also analyze an inexact version based on relaxed descent conditions. We establish the global convergence of the inexact algorithm in the nonconvex setting and prove a linear convergence rate under an error-bound condition. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned $\ell_1$-regularized, structured $\ell_1$-regularized, and linearly constrained problems demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [153] [Separation, Constraint Qualifications, and Cycling in Outer Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26897)
*Erik Tamm,Jan Kronqvist*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了外近似算法收敛性中的两个关键假设——约束规格和子问题精确求解，揭示了它们与循环问题的联系，并提出了基于扩展切割平面的回退策略，在放宽假设下实现了有限收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外近似算法在凸混合整数非线性规划中广泛应用，但文献中很少讨论其收敛性所依赖的某些假设（约束规格成立、子问题可精确求解）在实际中是否总能满足。

Method: 分析约束规格失效和近似求解导致循环的机理；证明当Slater条件接近违反时，存在靠近精确解的点使得分离失败；提出在检测到循环时使用扩展切割平面作为回退策略。

Result: 约束规格失效或子问题近似求解均可能导致分离失败和循环；扩展切割平面回退策略在放宽的假设下保证了算法的有限收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了外近似算法的收敛理论，使其在约束规格不一定成立、子问题不一定精确求解的情况下仍能有限收敛。

Abstract: The outer approximation algorithm is a widely used method for solving convex mixed-integer nonlinear programs. While the algorithm is well established in theory and practice, certain assumptions underlying its convergence are rarely discussed in the literature. In this paper, we examine two such assumptions: that a constraint qualification holds at the optimal solution of each nonlinear programming subproblem, and that these subproblems can be solved exactly. We argue that both assumptions are connected to the issue of cycling, by which we mean that the same integer assignment reappears in successive iterations of the algorithm. When a constraint qualification fails, separation of the current iterate from the feasible set is not guaranteed, which can cause the algorithm to stall. When the nonlinear programming subproblem is solved only approximately, separation may likewise fail, and we show that high precision can be required in certain cases. To formalize the connection between these issues, we prove that when Slater's condition is nearly violated, a point close to the exact solution can be found at which separation fails. Furthermore, within the outer approximation algorithm, we propose to use extended cutting planes as a fallback strategy when cycling is detected. We prove that this approach yields a finitely convergent algorithm under relaxed assumptions regarding constraint qualifications, thereby generalizing the convergence theory of the outer approximation algorithm.

</details>


### [154] [Numerical Approximation for Path-Dependent McKean-Vlasov Control with Non-Asymptotic Error Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27181)
*Olivier Bokanowski,Jean-Francois Chassagneux,Xinyu Li,Christoph Reisinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对路径依赖的McKean-Vlasov控制问题，提出了一个统一的自适应与学习框架，包括Euler离散化方案（误差$O(h^{1/4})$）、离散动态规划原理、粒子系统近似以及神经网络策略梯度算法，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径依赖的McKean-Vlasov（MKV）控制模型能够描述具有历史依赖动力学和成本的大规模交互群体，但连续时间下该类问题的求解存在困难，亟需一个统一的近似与学习框架。

Method: 1）采用分段常数控制的Euler离散化方案，达到$O(h^{1/4})$的非渐近误差；2）建立离散动态规划原理，证明开环控制与历史依赖反馈控制之间的值等价性；3）引入交互粒子系统逼近连续时间值函数，总误差界为$O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$；4）提出基于路径特征的全可实现神经网络策略梯度方法。

Result: Euler离散化非渐近误差$O(h^{1/4})$；粒子系统总误差$O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$；路径依赖线性二次型基准上的数值实验验证了算法有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为连续时间路径依赖MKV控制问题提供了首个统一的近似与学习框架，理论分析与数值实验均表明方法有效。

Abstract: Path-dependent McKean--Vlasov (MKV) control models large interacting populations with history-dependent dynamics and costs. This paper develops a unified approximation-and-learning framework for continuous time path-dependent MKV problem under open-loop controls. First, an Euler discretization scheme with piecewise-constant controls is shown to achieve a non-asymptotic error of $O(h^{1/4})$. Second, we establish a discrete dynamic programming principle and prove value equivalence between open-loop and history-dependent feedback controls, enabling optimization on a reduced filtration. Third, an interacting particle system is introduced to approximate the continuous-time value, yielding an overall error bound of $O(h^{1/4}) + O(M^{-γ})$ for $M$ particles and an explicitly given $γ> 0$. Finally, we propose a fully implementable neural-network policy-gradient method using pathwise features. Numerical experiments, including a path-dependent linear-quadratic benchmark, demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm.

</details>


### [155] [Benign Landscape of Quadratic Programs with Orthogonality Constraints and Its Application to Heteroscedastic Probabilistic PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27189)
*Peng Wang,Po Chen,Rujun Jiang,Laura Balzano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了带正交约束的齐次二次规划（QPOC）的优化景观，证明了其每个临界点要么是全局最大化点，要么是严格鞍点。将该理论应用于异方差概率PCA（HePPCA），证明了其总体版本具有良性优化景观，且样本版本在样本量足够大时以高概率继承这些性质，从而为基于收缩的优化方法达到全局解的局部线性收敛提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解带正交约束的齐次二次规划的优化景观，并将理论应用于异方差概率PCA，为其优化方法的收敛性提供理论保证。

Method: 通过闭式刻画临界点集，推导全局最优性的充要条件，证明每个非最优临界点具有正曲率方向。将QPOC理论应用于HePPCA的总体版本和样本版本，结合严格鞍点回避理论分析优化景观。

Result: QPOC中每个临界点要么是全局最大化点，要么是严格鞍点；HePPCA总体版本具有良性优化景观且全局最大化点附近满足局部测地线强凹性；样本量足够大时样本版本以高概率继承这些性质。

Conclusion: QPOC和HePPCA具有良性优化景观，基于收缩的优化方法能够以局部线性收敛速度达到全局解，理论结果通过数值实验得到验证。

Abstract: In this work, we study the optimization landscape of homogeneous quadratic programs with orthogonality constraints (QPOC) and apply the resulting theory to heteroscedastic probabilistic PCA (HePPCA). For QPOC, we establish a complete characterization of the benign optimization landscape by showing that every critical point is either a global maximizer or a strict saddle point. Our analysis builds on a closed-form characterization of the critical point set, from which we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for global optimality and show that every non-optimal critical point has a direction of positive curvature. As an application, we show that the population version of HePPCA is a special instance of QPOC and therefore has a benign optimization landscape; moreover, it satisfies local geodesic strong concavity near every global maximizer. We furthermore prove that, when the sample size is sufficiently large, the sample version of HePPCA inherits these favorable properties with high probability. Together with existing theory on avoiding strict saddle points, our results provide a theoretical justification for the observed local linear convergence of retraction-based optimization methods to global solutions for both QPOC and HePPCA. Finally, we present numerical experiments to corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [156] [Optimizing Accessibility and Maximum Travel Times in Transit Networks: The Hub Covering Location Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27196)
*Carmen-Ana Domínguez-Bravo,Elena Fernández,Armin Lüer-Villagra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种优化模型，用于支持综合交通系统设计中的决策，旨在改善城市出行。模型考虑用户出行由短距离（私人交通至公交站点）和长距离（公共交通网络）两部分组成，通过限制短途出行时间确保可达性，限制长途出行时间确保系统效率。提出了两种混合整数线性规划公式和一种元启发式算法，并通过计算实验评估其效率，分析了参数影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改善城市出行，支持综合交通系统设计中的决策制定，解决用户可达性与系统效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: 建立了优化模型，将用户出行分解为短距离（私人交通）和长距离（公共交通网络）两段；通过限制短途出行时间确保可达性，限制覆盖半径内用户使用系统；提出了两种混合整数线性规划（MILP）公式和一种元启发式（matheuristic）求解方法，并通过计算实验评估性能。

Result: 通过计算实验评估了所提方法的效率，分析了求解结构以及各参数对系统设计的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的优化模型和求解方法能够有效支持综合交通系统设计，通过参数分析揭示了可达性与系统效率之间的平衡关系。

Abstract: This work introduces an optimization model to support decision-making in the design of integrated transit systems, with the aim of improving urban mobility. We assume that daily user trips consist of two types of segments: short segments where users apply their own transport between their homes or workplaces and the public transit access points, and a longer segment on the transportation network. Users' accessibility is ensured by imposing an upper limit on the travel times of the short segments. We assume that only users within a given coverage radius will employ the system. On the other hand, system effectiveness is ensured by limiting travel time on the transportation network. Two mixed-integer linear programming formulations and a matheuristic procedure are proposed, and their efficiency is evaluated through computational experiments. The results allow us to study the structure of the solutions obtained and analyze the effects of the parameters considered.

</details>


### [157] [Conjecture About Arbitrary Even-Order Convexity of $w$-Optimization of the Split CIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27260)
*Hao Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据融合中分裂协方差交集滤波器（Split CIF）的w-优化问题，指出其具有二阶凸性和四阶凸性，并进一步提出猜想：该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性（即二阶、四阶、六阶等），尽管高阶凸性对实际应用意义有限，但该猜想可能激发有价值的数学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从实际应用出发，w-优化问题作为Split CIF的理论基础，已知具有二阶和四阶凸性，可保证滤波器的快速实现。作者进一步基于观察提出更高阶凸性的猜想，旨在推动相关数学理论的系统研究。

Method: 基于对w-优化问题的观察与分析，提出该问题可能具有任意偶数阶凸性的数学猜想，并在论文中对该猜想进行了详细阐述。

Result: 已知w-优化问题具有二阶凸性（即传统凸性）和四阶凸性；猜想其可能具有任意偶数阶凸性（包括六阶、八阶等更高阶）。

Conclusion: 尽管更高阶凸性本身对Split CIF的实际应用可能意义不大，但该猜想有望促进对系统性数学技巧的研究，具有潜在的学术价值。

Abstract: The $w$-optimization problem is a mathematical problem abstracted from a useful tool for general data fusion rooted in various engineering tasks. Purely from the perspective of practical applications for this useful tool namely the split covariance intersection filter (Split CIF), it is sufficient to know that the $w$-optimization problem enjoys not only the second-order convexity (namely convexity in conventional sense) but also the fourth-order convexity, thanks to which a guaranteed fast implementation of the Split CIF can be realized. On the other hand, based on certain observations and analysis, the author proposes a conjecture concerning the $w$-optimization problem for further mathematical study. The conjecture is that the $w$-optimization problem has arbitrary even-order convexity, or in other words, it has the second-order convexity, the fourth-order convexity, the sixth-order convexity, and so on -- As mentioned above, whether the $w$-optimization problem has higher-order convexity or not might be of little interest itself for practical applications, yet the author believes that the conjecture would stimulate study on systematic mathematical techniques that are potentially interesting and valuable -- This paper presents the conjecture in details.

</details>


### [158] [A Verification Theorem for an Optimal Control Problem Governed by the Convective Brinkman--Forchheimer Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27312)
*Sagar Gautam,Manil T. Mohan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二维和三维环面上由对流Brinkman--Forchheimer方程描述的最优控制问题建立了验证定理，推导了庞特里亚金最大值原理，并通过强解理论、非线性吸收项的精细处理、负阶Sobolev空间新估计等方法，在二维和三维超临界及临界情形下严格证明了反馈控制表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes类型流体模型的最优控制验证问题研究较少，而Brinkman--Forchheimer系统的验证定理需要全新的正则性框架，传统二维Navier-Stokes方法不足以处理该系统的非线性吸收项和三维情形下的挑战。

Method: 采用强解理论，对非线性吸收项进行精细处理，利用负阶Sobolev空间的新估计，以及在更强拓扑下的连续依赖性估计。三维情形下分别处理超临界情形(r∈(3,5])和临界情形(r=3，条件2βμ≥1)。

Result: 成功在二维和三维（包括超临界和临界情形）中建立了验证定理，严格证明了庞特里亚金最大值原理和反馈控制表征。

Conclusion: 本文为对流Brinkman--Forchheimer系统的最优控制问题提供了完整的验证框架，克服了传统Navier-Stokes方法在该问题中的局限性，拓展了流体控制问题的理论范围。

Abstract: This article establishes a verification theorem for an optimal control problem governed by the two- and three-dimensional convective Brinkman--Forchheimer equations on the $d$-dimensional torus, $d\in\{2,3\}$:
  $$\frac{\partial\mathfrak{u}}{\partial t}
  -μΔ\mathfrak{u}
  +(\mathfrak{u}\cdot\nabla)\mathfrak{u}
  +α\mathfrak{u}
  +β|\mathfrak{u}|^{r-1}\mathfrak{u}
  +\nabla\mathfrak{p}
  =\boldsymbol{f},
  \qquad
  \nabla\cdot\mathfrak{u}=0,$$
  where $μ,α,β>0$ and $r\in[1,\infty)$. We derive the Pontryagin maximum principle and develop a verification framework for the associated control problem, a topic that has received comparatively little attention for fluid models of Navier--Stokes type. A major challenge in establishing the verification theorem and the corresponding feedback characterization for the CBF system is that the analysis requires a substantially different regularity framework from that used for the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations. In particular, the present approach relies on strong solution theory, a delicate treatment of the nonlinear absorption term, novel estimates in negative-order Sobolev spaces, and continuous dependence estimates in stronger topologies, especially in the three-dimensional setting. A distinctive feature of the present work is that the verification framework is developed not only in two dimensions, but also in the three-dimensional supercritical regime, corresponding to $r\in(3,5]$, and in the critical case $r=3$ under the condition $2βμ\geq1$. Consequently, the feedback characterization and verification arguments can be rigorously justified in both two and three dimensions.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Hochschild (co)homology and cyclic homology via a graded Euler characteristic with applications to higher preprojective algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26255)
*Jon Wallem Anundsen,Mads Hustad Sandøy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Etingof和Eu计算预投射代数Hochschild(上)同调的方法到更高次预投射代数，并应用于2-表示有限代数的更高次预投射代数，发现只需知道中心和零次Hochschild同调的 graded 向量空间结构即可推导出所有其他次数的Hochschild(上)同调和循环同调的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Etingof和Eu的方法利用了ADE型预投射代数的强结构特征，但仅适用于该特定类型。本文旨在将此方法推广到更高次预投射代数，以处理更广泛的代数类。

Method: 推广Etingof和Eu基于graded Euler characteristic和graded Cartan矩阵的方法到更高次预投射代数；通过分析中心和零次Hochschild同调的graded向量空间结构，推导其他次数的同调结构。

Result: 成功将方法推广到更高次预投射代数；对于由A型表示有限遗传代数的张量积得到的2-表示有限代数的更高次预投射代数，证明了只需知道中心和零次Hochschild同调即可确定所有其他次数的Hochschild(上)同调和循环同调的结构。

Conclusion: 本文的方法推广有效，且对特定类别的代数展现了计算Hochschild(上)同调及循环同调的简化途径，显著降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Computing the structure of the Hochschild (co)homology and the cyclic homology of an algebra can be hard work, but Etingof and Eu showed that it can be done surprisingly easily for preprojective algebras of ADE Dynkin type, at least if one only wants to know the graded vector space structure of each Hochschild cohomology group. Their method is based on exploiting strong structural features of such a preprojective algebra via a graded Euler characteristic that can computed using the algebra's graded Cartan matrix. In this paper, we present a generalization of the method used by Etingof and Eu to higher preprojective algebras. We also apply our generalization to the higher preprojective algebras of the 2-representation finite algebras that arise as tensor products of representation finite hereditary algebras of type A. For this, it turns out to be enough to know the graded vector space structure of the center and the zeroth Hochschild homology to be able to deduce the graded vector space structure of the Hochschild (co)homology and the cyclic homology in all other degrees.

</details>


### [160] [Quantum Harish-Chandra bimodules at roots of unity and affine Hecke category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26331)
*Trung Vu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子群在q为奇次单位根时的Harish-Chandra双模范畴，并将其与仿射Soergel双模范畴和非交换Springer解联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harish-Chandra双模范畴最早出现在曲面的拓扑量子场论研究中，本文旨在深入理解该范畴在特定参数（q为奇次单位根）下的结构性质。

Method: 通过建立Harish-Chandra双模范畴与仿射Soergel双模范畴以及非交换Springer解之间的联系来进行研究。

Result: 建立了Harish-Chandra双模范畴与仿射Soergel双模范畴及非交换Springer解之间的关联。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解量子群在奇次单位根处的表示理论提供了新的视角，并揭示了这些范畴之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The category of Harish-Chandra bimodules for quantum groups was first appeared in the works about topological quantum field theory of surfaces. In this paper, we study this category when the quantum parameter q is an odd order root of unity. We relate the category to the category of affine Soergel bimodules and to non-commutative Springer resolution.

</details>


### [161] [Brick infinite algebras admit infinitely many non-$τ$-rigid bricks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27063)
*Kaveh Mousavand,Charles Paquette*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：如果代数A上除有限个brick外都是τ-rigid的，则A是brick-finite的。等价地，任何brick-infinite代数都有无穷多个非τ-rigid的brick。这验证了一个开放猜想的一个较弱版本，并为第二brick-Brauer-Thrall猜想提供了新的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到brick与τ-rigid模之间基础性相互作用的启发，旨在验证一个开放猜想的较弱版本：若A上（几乎）所有brick都是rigid的，则A是brick-finite的。同时为了推进与第二brick-Brauer-Thrall猜想相关的一系列挑战性问题。

Method: 在有限维代数的一般性框架下，不依赖任何tameness假设，通过分析brick与τ-rigid模之间的结构关系，建立了brick-finiteness与τ-rigid性质之间的充分性条件。

Result: 完全证明了：若代数A上除有限个brick外都是τ-rigid的，则A必为brick-finite。等价叙述是：任何brick-infinite代数必然存在无穷多个非τ-rigid的brick。由于τ-rigid蕴含rigid，这验证了相关开放猜想的较弱版本。该结果还解决了一个此前仅对E-tame代数已知的问题。

Conclusion: 本研究在无tameness假设的条件下强化了先前的结果，为brick-Brauer-Thrall猜想系列问题提供了重要进展，建立了brick-finiteness与τ-rigid性质之间更深刻的联系。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finite dimensional algebra over an algebraically closed field. Motivated by some foundational interactions between bricks and $τ$-rigid modules, we prove, in full generality, that if all but finitely many bricks of the algebra $A$ are $τ$-rigid, then $A$ is brick-finite. Equivalently, any brick-infinite algebra admits infinitely many bricks which are not $τ$-rigid. Because $τ$-rigidity implies rigidity, our result verifies a weaker version of an open conjecture which states that if (almost) all bricks over $A$ are rigid, then $A$ should be brick-finite. In retrospect, this work strengthens some previous results and contributes to the recent studies of a series of challenging problems, all tied to the $2$nd brick-Brauer-Thrall conjecture. More specifically, without any tameness assumption, we settle a question that was previously known only for $E$-tame algebras.

</details>


### [162] [On some components of $L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$ associated with rooted trees for symmetrizable Kac-Moody algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27197)
*Rekha Biswal,Patrick Polo*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称化Kac-Moody代数$L(ρ)$的晶体$B(ρ)$中一类新的$ρ$-dominant元素，这些元素由Dynkin子图$I$（仅含简单边且无长度≥3的环）的子集$D$及根顶点选择所决定，并证明了它们可导出张量积$L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$的新分解分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Kac-Moody代数的表示论中，寻找张量积分解的新分支是一个基本问题。作者通过晶体的组合结构，系统构造出$B(ρ)$中满足特定权重条件的$ρ$-dominant元素，从而丰富了对$L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$分解的认识。

Method: 首先选取Dynkin图的一类子图$I$（仅含简单边、无长度≥3的环），对每个子集$D\subset I$（满足$λ_{D,I}=2ρ-β_I-β_D$ dominant），基于每个连通分量中根顶点的选择，定义特定的晶体元素$π_{D,I}$（权重为$λ_{D,I}-ρ$），然后证明它们是$ρ$-dominant的。

Result: 构造了一族新的$ρ$-dominant元素$π_{D,I}\in B(ρ)$，从而给出了张量积$L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$分解的新分支。

Conclusion: 该构造为理解$L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$的晶体结构提供了新的组合工具，且所得到的元素族依赖于子图$I$的连通分量根顶点选择，展现了丰富的组合结构。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{g}$ be a symmetrizable Kac-Moody algebra over $\mathbb{C}$ and let $L(ρ)$ be the irreducible integrable $\mathfrak{g}$-module with highest weight $ρ$. Let $I$ be a subgraph of the Dynkin diagram of $\mathfrak{g}$ which has only simple bonds and no cycle of length $\geq 3$. For every subset $D$ of $I$, denote by $β_D$ the sum of the simple roots corresponding to $D$. To every $D \subset I$ such that $λ_{D,I} = 2ρ- β_I - β_D$ is dominant, we associate certain elements $π_{D,I}$ of weight $λ_{D,I} {-} ρ$ in the crystal $B(ρ)$, which depend on the choice of a root vertex in each connected component of $I$. Then we prove that our elements are $ρ$-dominant
  elements of $B(ρ)$, hence provide new families of components
  of the tensor product $L(ρ)\otimes L(ρ)$.

</details>
